### [99] [What Does the Server See? Understanding Privacy Leakage from Large Language Models in Split Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23158)
*Mingyuan Fan,Yu Liu,Fuyi Wang,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActInv 攻击方法，证明大语言模型在 split inference 场景下存在严重的隐私泄露风险，并提出 PriPert 防御机制有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源受限设备上的部署面临挑战，split inference（分割推理）通过将模型分区部署在客户端和服务器之间来降低计算负担并增强隐私保护，但其隐私保护能力（特别是针对LLMs）尚未被充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 提出 ActInv，通过解决中间激活匹配问题来重建客户端输入。2. 开发 Perturbation Amplification Factor (PAF) 度量指标，量化各层对重建攻击的内在抵抗力。3. 基于 PAF 分析，设计 PriPert 防御机制，通过校准扰动方向以在反向传播中最大化重建误差。

Result: ActInv 在常见扰动防御（高斯噪声注入、激活稀疏化）下仍能实现高保真重建。PAF 分析表明各层隐私脆弱性不均，部分层高度易泄露，部分层天然具有抵抗力。通过校准扰动方向可显著提升防御效果。

Conclusion: Split inference 存在严重隐私泄露风险，且简单扰动防御不足。基于 PAF 指导的 PriPert 在隐私、效用和计算开销的综合评估中表现有效。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) on resource-constrained devices remains challenging, spurring interest in split inference, where models are partitioned between client and server to reduce computational burden and enhance privacy by transmitting only intermediate activations. However, the privacy-preserving capabilities of split inference, particularly in the context of LLMs, have not been exhaustively investigated. To fill this gap, we introduce ActInv, which solves an intermediate activation matching problem to reconstruct the client's input. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that ActInv achieves high-fidelity reconstructions, even in the presence of common perturbation-based defenses such as Gaussian noise injection and activation sparsification. To systematically understand this vulnerability, we develop Perturbation Amplification Factor (PAF), a metric for quantifying a layer's inherent resistance to reconstruction. Our analysis reveals that privacy vulnerability is not uniform across layers, with some layers being highly susceptible to leakage while others offer natural resistance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that defense effectiveness can be significantly improved by calibrating perturbation directions to maximize reconstruction error during backpropagation. Building on these insights, we design PriPert and conduct comprehensive evaluations, covering privacy, utility, and computational overhead, to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [100] [PoisonForge: Task-Level Targeted Poisoning Benchmark for Instruction-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23168)
*Luze Sun,Anshuman Suri,Harsh Chaudhari,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PoisonForge基准，系统评估了在微调大语言模型时，通过任务级投毒（在1000条微调样本中仅插入10条恶意样本）即可使12个模型中11个达到70%以上攻击成功率，且不影响非目标任务性能。分析表明投毒设计选择（而非模型规模）是攻击成功的主要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际微调场景中，数据集来自不可信来源，攻击者可能通过任务级投毒操纵模型输出。现有研究缺乏对这类威胁的系统性基准评估，以及影响攻击成功因素的深入分析。

Method: 构建PoisonForge基准，沿四个维度（偏见类型、投毒模式、实体出现次数、目标输出长度）参数化威胁；在1%投毒预算下评估5个系列12个开放权重模型（2B~32B参数）。

Result: 1%投毒率下（1000条中10条），11/12模型在最脆弱配置下ASR超过70%；非目标任务泄露低于0.5%；模型在标准基准上表现正常。实体多次出现提升ASR；最优投毒模式取决于目标实体的语义结构；ASR随输出长度单调下降。

Conclusion: 投毒设计选择（而非模型规模）是攻击成功的主要决定因素；相关性分析和风险预测模型证实这些模式可泛化到预测新任务上的攻击成功。

Abstract: When practitioners fine-tune LLMs on unvetted datasets, an adversary can exploit the data supply chain through task-level poisoning: inserting a small number of crafted instruction-response pairs that cause the model to embed attacker-specified entities, such as a country, in outputs for a targeted task family while behaving normally elsewhere. We introduce PoisonForge, a benchmark that parameterizes this threat along four dimensions (bias type, poisoning mode, appearance count, and target output length) and evaluates 12 open-weight models (from 2B to 32B parameters) across five families under a primarily 1% poison budget. With only 10 poisoned examples among 1,000 fine-tuning examples, 11 of 12 models exceed a 70% attack success rate (ASR) in their most vulnerable configuration. Meanwhile, unintended leakage to non-target tasks remains below 0.5%, and models perform well on standard benchmarks. We analyze in detail the factors contributing to attack success. We observe that multiple appearances of an entity increase the ASR, the optimal poisoning mode depends on the semantic structure of the target entity, and ASR drops monotonically with the task output length. A correlation analysis and risk prediction model confirm that poisoning design choices, rather than model scale, are the primary causes of attack success, and that these patterns generalize to predict attack success on new tasks. We release all configurations, pipelines, and analysis code to support reproducible comparisons.

</details>


### [101] [Robust LLM Watermarking with Minimal Semantic Distortion for IP Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23175)
*Kieu Dang,Phung Lai,NhatHai Phan,Yelong Shen,Ruoming Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SAFESEAL，一种基于密钥条件的水印框架，通过密钥条件 Tournament 采样保留命名实体并替换同义词，结合密钥条件对比检测器，在保持文本质量的同时实现高检测率（98.2%）和鲁棒性，并发布了首个公开水印排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大语言模型面临知识产权侵犯风险，现有水印方法存在语义失真、事实不一致、对抗攻击脆弱性以及跨提供方/多用户场景下密钥条件水印检测能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出SAFESEAL框架：(1) 密钥条件 Tournament 采样机制，保留命名实体并用上下文感知同义词替换；(2) 密钥条件对比检测器，联合编码文本和密钥实现特定提供方水印验证；(3) 通过轻量模型、批处理和并行化降低延迟，并推导了效用-可检测性权衡的理论界。

Result: 在多个指标上超越基线：BERTScore 0.983，实体相似度 0.963，检测率 98.2%，人类评分最高，延迟与最快基线相当。

Conclusion: SAFESEAL有效平衡了可检测性、效用和鲁棒性，并发布首个公开水印排行榜和交互式演示以推动社区发展。

Abstract: Proprietary large language models (LLMs) face risks of intellectual property (IP) violation, as adversaries can replicate an LLM by collecting input-output pairs to train a surrogate model, causing financial setbacks. Watermarks offer a promising defense to verify ownership, but existing methods often struggle with semantic distortion, factual inconsistency, and adversarial attacks. In addition, key-conditioned watermarks for provider-specific detection, especially in cross-provider and multi-user scenarios, remain largely underexplored. To address these challenges, we propose SAFESEAL, a novel key-conditioned watermarking framework that achieves strong detectability with minimal impact on model utility, effectively balancing detectability, utility, and robustness. SAFESEAL preserves named entities while substituting linguistic terms with context-aware synonyms through a key-conditioned Tournament sampling mechanism, maintaining semantic fidelity and factual consistency. For detection, we introduce a key-conditioned contrastive detector that jointly encodes the text and key, enabling provider-specific and robust watermark verification. We derive theoretical bounds on the utility-detectability trade-off and significantly reduce latency through lightweight models, batching, and parallelism. Extensive experiments show that SAFESEAL outperforms baselines in utility, detectability, and robustness, achieving a BERTScore of 0.983, entity similarity of 0.963, a 98.2% detection rate, and the highest human ratings for text quality and content preservation, with latency comparable to the fastest baseline. To promote transparency and community-driven progress, we release the first public watermark leaderboard and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [102] [Prompt Overflow: What the Guardrail Inspects Is Not What the Model Infers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23196)
*Yuanbo Zhou,Changjia Zhu,Junyu Wang,Xu He,Yan Zhai,Kun Sun,Mingkui Wei,Junjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并利用护栏模型与下游LLM之间上下文窗口大小的不匹配，提出了一种Prompt Overflow Attack，通过将恶意指令碎片化并穿插良性填充内容组成超长prompt，使护栏模型各分段检查均显示无害，但下游LLM仍能执行完整恶意指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注语义层面的对抗性输入，但护栏模型处理超长prompt时的截断或分段检查机制存在的安全隐患尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Prompt Overflow Attack，将恶意指令碎片化并与良性填充内容交错排列，构造超长prompt，使护栏模型的有限检查窗口无法识别恶意内容，而下游LLM的更大上下文窗口可重构并执行完整指令。

Result: 在Meta Llama Prompt Guard、IBM Granite Guardian和基于DeBERTa的检测器上的系统评估表明，原本在短上下文中可被可靠检测的prompt，经对抗性构造为超长输入后可成功绕过护栏模型，同时下游LLM仍可完全执行。

Conclusion: 揭示了护栏模型与LLM上下文窗口不匹配带来的安全盲区，并提出潜在防御策略和缓解方向以增强护栏模型的安全性。

Abstract: Guardrail models (a.k.a. safety checkers) are widely deployed to screen user inputs before they reach large language models (LLMs), serving as a primary defense against prompt injection attacks. Due to strict context constraints, these models handle overlength prompts through truncation or segmentation-based inspection. While prior work has focused on semantic adversarial inputs, the security implications of these long-input processing mechanisms remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we identify a critical blind spot arising from the mismatch between the limited inspection windows of guardrail models and the substantially larger context inference windows of downstream LLMs. We introduce a novel Prompt Overflow Attack, which exploits this mismatch by fragmenting malicious instructions and interleaving them with benign filler content across an overlong prompt, such that no individual inspected segment appears malicious while the full context remains actionable to the LLM. Through a systematic evaluation against state-of-the-art guardrail models, including Meta Llama Prompt Guard, IBM Granite Guardian, and DeBERTa-based detectors, we demonstrate that prompts reliably detected in short-context settings can evade guardrail models once adversarially manipulated into over-length inputs, yet remain fully actionable by downstream LLMs. We further propose potential defense strategies and outline mitigation directions to strengthen guardrail models.

</details>


### [103] [AI Security Research Should Better Incentivize Defense Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23448)
*Youqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AI安全研究中存在攻击研究多于防御研究的不平衡现象，且攻击论文常在有利条件下评估使威胁显得更严重，而防御则面临更严格标准，导致文献中漏洞多但可用防护少，因此需要更好地激励防御研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出现有AI安全研究存在攻击与防御研究的不平衡，攻击论文数量远超防御论文，且评估标准不对称，导致防御研究被忽视。

Method: 通过梳理联邦学习、语音识别、成员推理、大语言模型等子领域的相关学术论文，比较攻击与防御研究的数量和评估标准。

Result: 发现各子领域均存在攻击研究多于防御研究的偏斜比例，攻击论文在有利条件下评估，防御则面临更苛刻标准，导致防御研究难以落地部署。

Conclusion: AI安全研究应更好地激励防御研究，以纠正当前不平衡现状。

Abstract: This work examines an imbalance in artificial intelligence (AI) security research: the field tends to produce more work on attacking AI systems than on defending them. Drawing on related academic papers, we find biased attack-to-defense ratios across subfields, including federated learning, speech recognition, membership inference, large language models, etc. The imbalance possibly means far beyond a simple count: attack papers are routinely evaluated under favorable conditions that make threats look more severe than they are in practice, while defenses are held to a stricter standard that few can meet. The result is a literature rich in demonstrated vulnerabilities and thin on usable and deployed protections. We thus argue that AI security research should better incentivize defense research.

</details>


### [104] [When Youth Enter the Algorithmic Wild: Discovering and Understanding Potentially Harmful Teen Videos on Douyin and Kwai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23598)
*Shaoxuan Zhou,Yafei Sun,Jing Zhang,Xianghang Mi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究构建了首个大规模、行为驱动的短视频平台有害内容测量框架PHTV-Scout，通过对186,727个视频和51,287条评论的分析，发现6.11%的视频为潜在有害内容（其中儿童性剥削影像占53.2%），揭示了有害内容通过隐蔽互动和语义伪装等规避手段传播的机制，并指出青少年模式虽能100%屏蔽但采用率仅30-41%的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台推荐算法可能向青少年推送有害内容，但其规模、机制和实际影响尚不清楚，现有测量方法面临推荐系统黑箱化、有害内容规避检测、爬虫无法模拟真实行为等挑战。

Method: 整合683名青少年离线调查与三模块在线管道：PHTV Hunter模拟青少年账户采集推荐流；PHTV Arbiter基于LoRA微调的多模态分类器（准确率94.29%，精确率96.41%）；PHTV Analyzer进行细粒度分类与影响评估。

Result: 6.11%视频为PHTV，儿童性剥削影像占53.2%；有害内容通过隐蔽互动（如诱拐评论、自我披露）和主动规避（语义伪装、噪声注入）扩散；青少年模式100%屏蔽但采用率仅30-41%；暴露驱动因素为平台算法和被动浏览而非用户身份。

Conclusion: 需从被动删除转向主动、以人为中心的安全防护范式，以应对青少年信息环境的脆弱性。

Abstract: Short-video platforms like Douyin and Kwai have become central to adolescent digital life, but they also risk exposing teens to algorithmically amplified harmful content. Despite its societal importance, the scale, mechanisms, and real-world impact of this exposure remain poorly understood. Measuring it is challenging: recommendation feeds are personalized black boxes, harmful content employs sophisticated evasion tactics, and naive crawlers fail to replicate authentic teen behavior. To bridge this gap, we propose PHTV-Scout, the first large-scale, behaviorally grounded measurement framework for Potentially Harmful Teen Videos (PHTVs). We integrate an offline survey of 683 adolescents with a tri-module online pipeline: (1) PHTV Hunter simulates teen accounts to collect recommendation feeds; (2) PHTV Arbiter, a LoRA-finetuned multimodal classifier, detects PHTVs with 94.29% accuracy and 96.41% precision; and (3) PHTV Analyzer performs fine-grained categorization and impact assessment. Over six months, we analyzed 186,727 videos and 51,287 comments, uncovering a troubling 6.11% PHTV prevalence--dominated by Child Sexual Exploitation Imagery (53.2%)--and revealing that harmful content thrives through covert interactions (e.g., grooming comments, self-disclosure) and active evasion (semantic camouflage, noise injection). Crucially, while Youth Mode blocks 100% of PHTVs, its low adoption (30-41%) leaves most teens unprotected. We further show that exposure is driven not by user identity but by regulation, platform algorithms, and even passive browsing, exposing the fragility of adolescent information environments. Our findings call for a paradigm shift from reactive takedowns to proactive, human-centered safeguards.

</details>


### [105] [CachePrune: Privacy-Aware and Fine-Grained KV Cache Sharing for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23640)
*Guanlong Wu,Zhaohan li,Yao Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Jianyu Niu,Ye Wu,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CachePrune是一种隐私感知的KV缓存共享机制，通过细粒度的token级缓存管理，在消除跨用户KV缓存共享带来的侧信道泄漏的同时，实现了4.5倍的TTFT降低和44%的缓存命中率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统中，跨用户的KV缓存共享虽能减少冗余计算，但引入了侧信道安全漏洞，攻击者可通过探测缓存是否命中来推断用户输入。现有的防御方法完全禁用共享，牺牲了大量复用潜力，因为提示词中往往包含大量与隐私无关的内容（如系统指令或公开材料）。

Method: CachePrune实现细粒度的token级缓存管理，通过精确高效地推导可复用的KV段，并在变长跨度上高效检索这些段，使得细粒度复用变得可行。该方法突破了现有粗粒度方案中固定大小或句子级别分块的限制。

Result: 在vLLM上实现的CachePrune，在三个数据集上的评估表明：消除了通过KV缓存复用侧信道带来的直接泄漏；相比最先进方法，TTFT降低4.5倍，缓存命中率提升44%。

Conclusion: CachePrune证明了在保障隐私安全的前提下，通过细粒度的token级缓存管理可以显著提升LLM推理系统的性能，为隐私感知的缓存共享提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on Key-Value (KV) caching to accelerate inference, and many serving systems further share the KV cache across users' requests to reduce redundant computation. While widely adopted, unrestricted cross-user sharing introduces side-channel vulnerabilities, allowing an adversary to infer user inputs by probing for cache reuse. Existing defenses disable sharing entirely to prevent leakage; yet such a coarse-grained strategy sacrifices substantial reuse potential, since prompts often include large portions of privacy-irrelevant segments, such as system instructions or publicly accessible materials. Building on this, we present CachePrune, a privacy-aware KV cache sharing mechanism that enables fine-grained reuse of KV entries across requests. Realizing such fine granularity requires token-level cache management, as reusable segments vary in length and position due to sensitivity masking, making reuse more complex than the fixed-size or sentence-level chunking used in existing coarse-grained schemes. Specifically, CachePrune makes fine-grained reuse practical by addressing two key challenges: accurately and efficiently deriving reusable KV segments and efficiently retrieving them over variable-length spans. We implement CachePrune on top of vLLM and evaluate it on three datasets, showing that it eliminates direct leakage through KV cache reuse side channels while reducing TTFT by 4.5x and increasing cache hit rates by 44% compared with state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [106] [Kernel-Based ReLU Approximation for Homomorphic Encryption-Compatible Privacy-preserving Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23641)
*Dimitrios Sygletos,Dimitra Papatsaroucha,Marios Choudetsanakis,Ilias Politis,Evangelos K. Markakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核函数与Jackson定理的ReLU二阶多项式逼近方法，使其能在同态加密（HE）约束下运行，从而为隐私保护的LLM推理迈出关键一步。实验表明该方法在多种场景下具有较好的逼近精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）仅支持加法和乘法运算，而LLM中广泛使用的ReLU等非线性激活函数无法直接应用于HE环境，这成为隐私保护NLP部署的关键障碍。本文旨在解决ReLU在HE框架下的可计算性问题。

Method: 基于Jackson定理，利用光滑核函数对ReLU进行逼近，并通过二阶多项式实现低乘法深度的近似，从而满足HE的运算约束。方法直接在预训练LLM的词嵌入上进行训练与评估。

Result: 在模拟数据、分词数据、深度学习模型及Transformer模型等多种场景下的评估显示，所提方法具有良好的逼近保真度，证明了其在安全隐私推理中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该核函数逼近方法能够有效支持HE环境下的ReLU计算，为隐私保护的LLM部署提供了可行方案，在多种NLP任务中展现出良好的安全推理能力。

Abstract: As privacy concerns in AI technologies continue to grow, Homomorphic Encryption (HE) offers a way to perform computations on encrypted data without the need of decryption during operations. However, HE is limited to addition and multiplication, making non-linear functions incompatible in their original form. This limitation has become more critical with the widespread use of Large Language Models (LLMs), where the non-linearity of activation functions such as the Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) poses challenges for deployment in privacy-preserving Natural Language Processing (NLP) settings. This paper proposes a kernel-based approximation of ReLU, enabling its use within HE-constrained settings and thus contributing a critical step toward supporting privacy-preserving LLMs. A smooth kernel-based function, mimicking ReLU, is approximated using a second-degree polynomial, inspired by Jackson's theorem, to achieve low multiplicative depth. The proposed method is trained and assessed directly on token embeddings from pre-trained LLMs and evaluated in various scenarios, from simulated and tokenized data to deep learning and transformer models. Results show improved approximation fidelity, supporting the method's suitability for secure and privacy-preserving inference in various tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Less Effort, Shorter Proofs: Reinforcement Learning for Security Protocol Analysis in Tamarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23643)
*Matthias Cosler,Cas Cremers,Bernd Finkbeiner,Mohamed Ghanem,Niklas Medinger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受AlphaZero和AlphaProof启发的强化学习框架，用于Tamarin协议验证工具的自动化证明搜索。该框架通过神经启发式引导蒙特卡洛树搜索，在16个案例研究中比Tamarin标准搜索找到更多自动证明，且生成的证明更短，减少了人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Tamarin和ProVerif等工具在分析EMV、5G、WPA2等复杂真实世界协议方面取得了显著成功，甚至能检测零日漏洞，但验证这些协议仍然耗时且困难，需要大量人工专业知识和努力。当前缺乏一种能够自动化和加速证明搜索过程的方法。

Method: 1) 为Tamarin开发了一个无状态API，充当经典强化学习环境；2) 采用受AlphaZero和AlphaProof启发的强化学习框架；3) 使用蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)引导搜索过程；4) 通过神经启发式学习完成子证明，指导搜索方向。

Result: 在16个案例研究上评估，涵盖经典协议模型到前沿出版物中的挑战性协议模型。该方法比Tamarin标准搜索找到更多自动证明，且生成的证明比标准启发式和人工设计的启发式都更短。

Conclusion: 该框架可直接用于辅助Tamarin用户进行活跃研究，减少了所需的人工工作量。提供的标准化接口使用户能够以编程方式与Tamarin交互。这项工作展示了将强化学习方法应用于Tamarin领域的潜力。

Abstract: Tools like Tamarin and ProVerif have achieved notable success in analyzing and verifying complex real-world protocols such as EMV, 5G, and WPA2, even detecting zero-day exploits. Despite these successes, verifying such protocols remains a time-consuming, challenging task, often requiring significant human effort and expertise. In this paper, we present a reinforcement learning (RL) framework inspired by AlphaZero and AlphaProof that implements a new style of proof search for Tamarin. We have developed a stateless API for Tamarin that acts as a classical RL environment. We guide a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) by a neural heuristic that learns from completed subproofs. We evaluate our framework on 16 case studies, ranging from classical protocol models to challenging state-of-the-art protocol models from recent publications. Our method finds more proofs automatically than Tamarin's standard search and produces shorter proofs than both the standard and human-engineered heuristics. Our pipeline is applicable out of the box to assist Tamarin users in active research, reducing the human effort required. Moreover, our standardized interface provides a programmatic way for users to interact with Tamarin. Finally, our work demonstrates the promising potential of adapting RL-based methods to the Tamarin domain.

</details>


### [108] [Validating Threat Modeling Results with the Help of Vulnerable Test Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23695)
*Oleksandr Adamov,Davide Fucci,Felix Viktor Jedrzejewski,Ricardo Britto,Nishrith Saini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于已知漏洞集的威胁建模验证方法，通过在两个故意存在漏洞的应用（AzureGoat和VulnBank）上比较LLM辅助工具ThreMoLIA与Microsoft Threat Modeling Tool（MTMT）的威胁发现能力。结果显示ThreMoLIA在漏洞覆盖率上优于MTMT，证明易受攻击的测试应用可作为评估威胁覆盖率的实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁建模结果的有效性验证困难，因为缺乏外部基准（oracle）来判断完整性。现有依赖专家参考模型的方法存在遗漏和分歧，需要一种互补的、更客观的验证方法。

Method: 采用漏洞驱动的验证方法：在已知漏洞集合的故意脆弱应用（AzureGoat和VulnBank）上应用威胁建模，测量能发现的相关漏洞数量。比较ThreMoLIA（LLM辅助威胁建模工具）与MTMT，两者输入均限于架构、数据流图及其描述。

Result: ThreMoLIA在两个系统上的漏洞覆盖率均高于MTMT。易受攻击的测试应用可作为评估威胁覆盖率的实用基准，补充专家验证方法。

Conclusion: 基于已知漏洞集的脆弱应用为威胁建模验证提供了可操作的基准，能够弥补专家评审的主观性和不完整性，是一种有效的互补验证策略。

Abstract: Validating threat modeling results remains difficult because completeness is hard to judge without an external oracle. Existing studies often rely on expert-produced reference models and other human baselines, but these can contain omissions or disagreements. This paper evaluates a complementary, vulnerability-grounded validation approach. We apply threat modeling to intentionally vulnerable applications with a known vulnerability set to measure the number of related vulnerabilities that can be discovered. We compare ThreMoLIA, an LLM-assisted threat modeling solution developed by our team, with the Microsoft Threat Modeling Tool (MTMT) across two vulnerable applications: AzureGoat and the Vulnerable Bank Application (VulnBank). The inputs to both tools are limited to architecture, data flow diagrams, and their descriptions. The results show that ThreMoLIA achieved higher vulnerability coverage on both systems. We show that vulnerable test applications provide a practical benchmark for assessing threat coverage and complement expert-based validation.

</details>


### [109] [A blueprint for constructing 3-pass AKE protocols under commitment-based models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23843)
*Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在承诺型AKE模型下，针对KA和KEM基础原语，构造了安全的三轮密钥交换协议，实现了单向认证，并给出了与已有四轮协议相同形式的安全性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于承诺的MT编译器构建了安全的四轮KA和KEM协议，本文旨在探索是否存在更高效的三轮协议，以降低通信轮次。

Method: 遵循承诺型MT认证器的核心思想，采用ad hoc方式分别构造了基于KA和KEM的三轮协议，并使用基于游戏的证明技术证明其在未认证模型下的SK安全性。

Result: 成功构造了两种原语下的三轮安全协议，安全性证明的界限形式与之前四轮协议相同，实现了三轮消息交换下的单向认证。

Conclusion: 在承诺型AKE模型中，存在安全的三轮KA和KEM协议，证明了通信效率可以进一步提升而不牺牲安全强度。

Abstract: The commitment-based AKE model provides a formal security framework for key exchange protocols that avoid long-term cryptographic material, achieving authentication through a final out-of-band verification of session-derived values. Within this model, secure KA-based and KEM-based protocols were previously constructed via a commitment-based MT compiler, yielding optimized 4-pass protocols. In this work, we show that 3-pass protocols secure under this model exist for both primitives. These protocols are constructed ad hoc, following the core ideas of the commitment-based MT authenticator, and their SK security in the unauthenticated model is proved using the same game-based techniques, achieving bounds of the same form as those previously achieved. The resulting protocols provide one-way authentication in three message exchanges.

</details>


### [110] [MELT: A Behavioral Trace Dataset for High-Risk Memecoin Launch Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13480)
*Sihao Hu,Selim Furkan Tekin,Yichang Xu,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MELT 是一个针对 Solana 链上模因币发行的首个行为轨迹数据集，包含 41k+ 个发行事件和 2 亿+ 笔交易，通过解析原始异构区块链数据为结构化行为记录（如交换、洗盘交易、转账、铸造），并引入捆绑轨迹数据揭示账户关联（平均 36.5% 代币由协调账户持有），提供 122 个行为特征和风险级别标注，用于检测高风险发行和 rug-pull 威胁，实验表明集成该检测的模因币选择策略可显著减少投资损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 rug-pull 检测方法无法捕捉模因币发行中 insider 如何积累、协调和退出仓位的新型高风险行为模式，需要从原始异构区块链数据中提取结构化行为轨迹来进行检测。

Method: 构建 MELT 数据集：从 Solana 链上收集 41k+ 模因币发行和 2 亿+ 笔交易，解析为结构化的行为类型记录；引入 bundle-trace 数据关联同一实体控制的账户；提取 122 个行为特征并进行风险标注；使用监督学习方法进行高风险发行检测。

Result: 平均 36.5% 的代币供应量由协调账户持有（揭示所有权集中伪装策略）；集成 ML 模型预测的简单模因币选择策略显著减少投资损失。

Conclusion: 行为轨迹可以有效转化为风险缓解手段，MELT 数据集为检测模因币高风险发行提供了全新的结构化分析基础。

Abstract: Launchpads have become the dominant mechanism for issuing memecoins, exposing investors to a new class of high-risk launches that existing rug-pull detection methods cannot capture. We argue that detecting these threats requires structured behavioral traces that underlie raw heterogeneous blockchain data, i.e., how insiders accumulate, coordinate, and unwind positions. To enable such analysis, we introduce MELT (MEmecoin Launch Trace, the first behavioral trace dataset for analyzing and detecting high-risk memecoin launches on Solana. MELT covers 41k+ memecoin launches with 200M+ transactions parsed into typed behavioral records that distinguish swaps, wash trades, transfers, and mints. Beyond per-account behaviors, MELT contributes bundle-trace data that links accounts controlled by the same entity, revealing that, on average, 36.5% of token supply is held by coordinated accounts, a concealment strategy that disguises the true ownership concentration from unsuspecting buyers. On top of these traces, MELT provides 122 behavioral features and risk-level annotations, enabling supervised learning at a population scale. We benchmark representative ML models on the high-risk launch detection task. Integrating their predictions into a simple memecoin selection strategy reduces investment loss significantly, demonstrating that behavioral traces can be translated into risk mitigation. Our dataset and code is available at https://github.com/git-disl/MELT.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Operationalizing Individual Fairness via Gradient Descent and Bradley-Terry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23145)
*Conlan Olson,Linjun Zhang,Zhun Deng,Pragya Sur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从三元组查询中学习马氏相似度度量的算法，用于实现个体公平性。算法采用谱初始化加梯度下降，在Bradley-Terry模型下提供理论收敛保证，并证明基于估计度量的个体公平性足以接近真实度量下的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体公平性要求"相似个体应被相似对待"，但实践中学习个体间的相似度度量非常困难，成为该公平性概念落地的关键障碍。

Method: 在Bradley-Terry成对比较模型下，通过三元组查询（"个体i与j还是k更相似？"）学习马氏距离度量；算法采用谱初始化步骤后接梯度下降，应对损失函数的非凸性。

Result: 算法能快速收敛到真实度量；基于估计度量的个体公平性足以保证接近真实度量下的公平性；实验验证了算法收敛性及下游公平预测器的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为个体公平性在实践中的落地提供了可行的度量学习方法，并建立了估计度量与真实度量之间公平性的理论联系，同时展示了在AI模型调优中的潜在应用。

Abstract: Individual fairness, the notion that "similar individuals should be treated similarly," provides a strong and flexible fairness guarantee for algorithmic decision makers. However, a barrier to implementing individual fairness in practice is the difficulty of learning the similarity metric over individuals. In this work, we present an algorithm for learning a Mahalanobis similarity metric from triplet queries of the form "is individual $i$ more similar to individual $j$ or $k$?" We work in the standard Bradley-Terry model for pairwise comparisons. Our algorithm consists of a spectral initialization step followed by gradient descent. We provide extensive theoretical guarantees on our algorithm, showing that it converges quickly to the ground truth metric despite the non-convexity of the loss in our model. Because our focus is on fairness, we also show that individual fairness with respect to an estimated metric is sufficient to achieve similar fairness with respect to the true metric. We also discuss potential applications of our work to AI model tuning. Finally, we present experimental results that demonstrate the convergence of our algorithm and the fairness performance of downstream fair predictors trained on our estimated metric.

</details>


### [112] [On the Stability of Spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich Flows and Their Implications for Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23879)
*Aratrika Mustafi,Soumya Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发展了球面Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK)梯度流的摄动理论，量化了势函数差异随时间的传播，并将其应用于差分隐私中的指数机制近似采样，提供了显式的纯DP和近似DP保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立SHK梯度流对势函数扰动的稳定性理论，为基于SHK几何的采样方法提供理论保障，并桥接采样理论与差分隐私领域。

Method: 发展SHK梯度流的摄动理论，比较两个不同势函数V和V'从共同初始化的流动，量化势差异随时间传播。利用均匀摄动界获得似然比和Rényi散度的无维数点态控制，并利用额外结构推导KL散度界。

Result: 得到了似然比和Rényi散度的无维数点态控制界，以及KL散度界。在差分隐私应用中，似然比控制提供了显式的时间依赖纯DP保证，KL界通过hockey-stick散度提供了近似DP证书，并推导了分离指数机制次优性与有限时间采样误差的效用界。

Conclusion: SHK梯度流的摄动理论成功建立了势函数差异与采样误差间的定量关系，为差分隐私中基于SHK的采样器提供了严格的隐私保证和效用分析。

Abstract: Gradient-flow sampling interprets a Gibbs distribution as the minimizer of an energy functional over probability measures and generates dynamics converging to this target. Under spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) geometry, the flow couples transport and reaction and coincides with birth-death Langevin dynamics. In this work, we develop a perturbation theory for SHK gradient flows. For two potentials $V$ and $V^{\prime}$, we compare the associated flows from a common initialization and quantify how potential discrepancies propagate over time. A uniform perturbation bound yields dimension-free, pointwise control of the log-likelihood ratio and Rényi divergence, while additional structure allows us to derive bounds for the KL divergence as well. We apply these results to approximate sampling for the exponential mechanism in differential privacy. The likelihood-ratio control provides explicit time-dependent Pure-DP guarantees for SHK-based samplers, while the KL bound yields Approximate-DP certificates via hockey-stick divergence. We also derive a utility bound separating intrinsic exponential-mechanism suboptimality from finite-time sampling error.

</details>


### [113] [Asymmetric Scaling Laws from Sparse Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23591)
*John Sous,Michael Winer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稀疏激活下的神经缩放定律模型，发现测试损失由训练输入中从未出现的稀有坐标主导，导致不同于密集模型的新瓶颈。推导了欠参数化和过参数化下的渐近总体损失，发现损失在插值阈值附近出现双下降峰，由两个不同缩放指数控制，间隙由稀疏程度决定。同时推导了计算最优边界、梯度下降稳定性缩放律，并证明该效应在非线性激活下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放定律主要关注密集模型，对稀疏激活下缩放行为的理论理解不足。本文旨在建立稀疏激活场景下缩放定律的理论模型，揭示其与密集模型不同的核心机制。

Method: 构建稀疏激活下的理论模型，分析训练输入中稀有坐标对测试损失的主导作用，推导欠参数化和过参数化下的渐近总体损失，分析双下降现象、计算最优边界、梯度下降稳定性，并扩展到非线性激活情形。

Result: 稀疏激活导致测试损失在插值阈值处出现双下降峰；损失曲线由两个不同缩放指数控制，间隙由稀疏度决定；计算最优边界倾向于增加数据集大小而非模型容量；推导了固定步梯度下降不稳定的概率缩放律；稀疏诱导效应在非线性激活下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 稀疏激活引入了一种新的缩放瓶颈，显著改变了神经缩放定律的行为模式，对模型设计和训练策略具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We introduce a model for neural scaling laws under sparse activations. In the model, test loss is often dominated by rare coordinates that are never observed in the training input. This mechanism induces a novel bottleneck absent from dense models. We derive the asymptotic population loss in both the underparameterized and overparameterized regimes, and show that the loss exhibits a double-descent peak near the interpolation threshold -- where the number of parameters is just sufficient to fit the training data -- resulting in a loss curve governed by two distinct scaling exponents -- one for the overparameterized regime and one for the underparameterized regime -- with a gap determined by the degree of sparsity. Additionally, we derive a compute-optimal frontier that favors increasing dataset size over model capacity under fixed compute budgets. We also analyze gradient-descent dynamics and identify a scaling law for the probability that fixed-step gradient descent becomes unstable. We further show that the sparsity-induced effect persists under nonlinear activations.

</details>


### [114] [LLM Sparsity Prior for Robust Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23102)
*Caleb Skinner,Yihan Guo,Meng Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP)方法，通过将LLM生成的权重整合到Spike-and-Slab及Spike-and-Slab Lasso模型的先验包含概率中，并引入两个可解释超参数（全局稀疏性和权重集中度）及其分层超先验，来动态降低低质量或误导性权重的影响，从而在高维变量选择中提升鲁棒性和预测精度。在急性肾损伤的私有医疗数据集上验证了该方法在低数据场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助变量选择方法（如LLM-Lasso）严重依赖LLM生成权重的质量，当权重不准确时性能显著下降，缺乏对权重质量的评估与鲁棒性机制。

Method: 1) 提出LLM生成权重的质量量化框架；2) 设计LSP方法，将LLM权重嵌入Spike-and-Slab和Spike-and-Slab Lasso的先验包含概率，通过全局稀疏性和权重集中度两个超参数调控；3) 采用分层超先验使模型能动态折扣低信息或误导性权重；4) 开发原则性提示工程策略。

Result: 在急性肾损伤的私有医疗数据集上，LSP相比基线方法提升了预测准确率，识别出临床相关但被基线忽略的特征，对提示词变化具有鲁棒性，在低数据场景下表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: LSP通过引入分层超先验机制，有效解决了LLM权重质量不可靠导致的鲁棒性问题，为LLM辅助高维变量选择提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable mechanism to elicit domain-informed prior information for high-dimensional variable selection. However, existing methods such as LLM-Lasso are sensitive to weight quality, with performance degrading substantially when LLM-generated weights are inaccurate. To address this challenge, we first introduce a framework for quantifying the quality of LLM-generated weights, enabling rigorous evaluation of LLM-informed methods across varying weight regimes. We then propose the LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP), which integrates LLM-generated weights into the prior inclusion probabilities of Spike-and-Slab and Spike-and-Slab Lasso models via two interpretable hyperparameters governing global sparsity and weight concentration. Hierarchical hyperpriors on these parameters allow the model to dynamically discount uninformative or misleading weights, improving robustness without sacrificing gains when weights are accurate. Finally, we develop principled prompt engineering strategies and validate the method on a private medical dataset studying Acute Kidney Injury. LSP improves prediction accuracy and identifies clinically relevant features missed by the baselines, with robustness to prompt variation and particular effectiveness in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [115] [Coupled Training with Privileged Information and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23268)
*Jiahao Shi,Omar Hagrass,Jason M. Klusowski*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合训练方法，在训练阶段利用额外信息（测试时不可用）来提升部署模型性能，同时避免了两阶段方法在额外信息噪声较大时导致准确率下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段方法（先用所有训练信息训练模型，再用其预测结果训练部署模型）在额外训练信息存在噪声或较弱时，会误导部署模型甚至降低准确率。因此需要一种能选择性利用额外信息的方法。

Method: 提出联合训练方法，同时学习两个模型（一个使用全量信息，一个仅使用测试时可用的输入），使部署模型仅在额外信息真正有帮助时才从中受益，而非继承其错误。并分析了一种适用于大规模高维模型的交替训练算法。

Result: 在合成数据和真实预测任务上的实验表明，该方法避免了传统两阶段方法的失败模式，稳健地优于标准两阶段基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合训练能够在额外训练信息存在噪声时仍提升部署模型性能，具有理论保证和实际有效性。

Abstract: In many prediction problems, we have extra information during training (for example, measurements that are expensive or slow to collect) that will not be available when the model is deployed. A common strategy is to first train a model that uses all training information, then use its predictions on unlabeled examples to train a second model that only uses the inputs available at test time. However, when the extra training-only information is weak or noisy, this Two-Stage approach can mislead the deployment model and even hurt accuracy. We propose a joint training method that learns the two models together, so the deployment model can benefit from the extra information only when it actually helps, instead of inheriting its mistakes. We provide guarantees that describe when joint training improves prediction accuracy and analyze a simple alternating training algorithm for large, high-dimensional models. Experiments on synthetic data and real-world prediction tasks show that our approach avoids these failures and robustly outperforms standard Two-Stage baselines.

</details>


### [116] [Concomitant DAG Learning: On the Roles of Noise Adaptivity, Sparsity, and Non-negativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23537)
*Gonzalo Mateos,Samuel Rey,Hamed Ajorlou,Mariano Tepper*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于将DAG结构学习重铸为连续、基于分数的邻接矩阵估计问题的教程，涵盖了从经典组合搜索到现代连续框架的发展，以及面向异方差性和分布偏移的噪声自适应联合估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果结构（DAG）推断面临两大障碍：一是无环约束带来的组合爆炸问题严重限制了可扩展性；二是潜在混淆或异质性噪声导致的不可识别性挑战。同时，干预实验可能不可行或存在伦理问题，因此需要从观测数据中推断DAG。

Method: 将DAG结构学习重铸为连续的、基于分数的估计问题，利用平滑的无环性刻画；进一步提出联合估计方法，同时推断稀疏因果结构和外生噪声水平，使估计器具有噪声自适应性。

Result: 通过连续优化框架克服了经典方法的组合复杂性瓶颈；噪声自适应方法提升了在异方差性和分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该教程为信号处理、因果推断、高维统计和可扩展图学习交叉领域的研究者提供了系统性的方法综述，并指出了在线、非线性和神经因果发现等新兴方向。

Abstract: Directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) constitute a central modeling tool to enable principled reasoning about cause-effect interactions in complex systems. However, since the causal structure underlying a group of variables is often unknown and interventions may be infeasible or ethically challenging to implement, there is a need to address the task of inferring DAGs from observational data. However, most classical structure identification approaches face two key obstacles: the combinatorial challenge of enforcing acyclicity, which severely limits scalability, and identifiability challenges arising from latent confounding or heterogeneous noise. This tutorial offers an overview of recent signal processing and optimization advances that address these issues by recasting DAG structure learning as a continuous, score-based estimation problem over adjacency matrices. We begin with a didactic introduction to structural equation models and the formulation of causal graph recovery, followed by a historical survey of score-based methods ranging from early combinatorial search schemes and greedy heuristics to modern continuous frameworks that leverage smooth characterizations of acyclicity. Building on this foundation, we describe concomitant DAG estimation methods that jointly infer sparse causal structure and exogenous noise levels, improving robustness under heteroscedasticity and distribution shifts by rendering the estimator noise adaptive. All in all, the tutorial introduces readers to challenges and opportunities for signal processing research at the crossroads of causal inference, high-dimensional statistics, and scalable graph learning, while outlining emerging directions including online, nonlinear, and neural causal discovery.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Kernel-Based MDPs from Episodic Preferential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23650)
*Nikola Pavlovic,Sattar Vakili,Qing Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在核马尔可夫决策过程（kernel MDP）框架下，对基于偏好反馈（而非数值奖励）的强化学习进行了严格的理论分析。学习者每轮部署两个策略，仅接收一个二元偏好标签（通过Bradley-Terry-Luce模型生成），论文开发了基于偏好的值估计和置信集方法，并证明了遗憾界随回合数次线性增长，即学习策略的值收敛到最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈通常以偏好形式（而非校准的数值奖励）出现，这促使了对偏好反馈强化学习（RLHF）的研究。本文旨在为这一重要但理论不足的领域提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 基于核马尔可夫决策过程（kernel MDP）假设，将偏好建模为Bradley-Terry-Luce模型（基于累积未观测奖励差值）。开发了基于偏好的值估计方法和置信集，专门针对回合末比较设计。

Result: 证明了高概率遗憾界随回合数次线性增长，即学习策略的值收敛到最优策略的值。

Conclusion: 在偏好反馈设置下，即使没有数值奖励信号，也能通过适当的方法实现策略的收敛性保证，为RLHF提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Human feedback often arrives as preferences rather than calibrated numeric rewards, motivating reinforcement learning from preferential feedback, also referred to as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). We present a rigorous theoretical study of preference-only learning in episodic kernel MDPs. In each episode, the learner deploys two policies from a common start state and receives a single binary label indicating which trajectory is preferred, modeled by a Bradley--Terry--Luce link on the difference of cumulative (unobserved) rewards. Under kernel-based assumptions on the reward and transition functions (one of the most general models amenable to theoretical analysis) we develop preference-based value estimation and confidence sets tailored to end-of-episode comparisons.We prove high-probability regret bounds that scale sublinearly in the number of episodes, implying that the value of the learned policy converges to that of the optimal policy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Cache Flow (LCF)，一种通过联合翻译和压缩KV缓存实现LLM间高效通信的方法。LCF适配器仅约C2C适配器4%的大小，在共享上下文设置中更准确；在不同上下文设置中，比基于文本的通信准确率提高23%，速度提升8.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体间文本通信延迟高、信息丢失严重；基于KV缓存翻译的方法（如C2C）适配器庞大且训练成本高，且要求目标上下文完全一致，不适用于智能体间上下文不同的实际通信场景。

Method: LCF通过联合翻译和压缩键值对，将适配器大小缩减至C2C的约4%；设计适配器仅传输目标模型缺失的新信息摘要，从而适应不同上下文场景。

Result: 13 MB的LCF适配器在共享上下文设置中比956 MB的C2C适配器更准确；在不同上下文设置中，比文本通信准确率高23%，速度快8.5倍。

Conclusion: LCF通过轻量级适配器和有针对性的信息传输，有效解决了LLM智能体间通信的效率和上下文差异问题，为高效智能体协作提供了新方案。

Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

</details>


### [119] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用逐层MLP更新的几何特征轨迹（而非最终概率或静态隐藏状态）进行不确定性量化，通过11个尺度不变几何特征的稀疏线性探针，在选择性弃权任务中优于最大softmax概率（MSP），最高提升21个AURC点，且探针系数具有可解释的几何意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSP虽廉价但常校准不良；现有探针方法将激活值视为静态快照，忽略表示形成的逐层轨迹。相似的最终表示可能源于截然不同的路径，而证据沿深度如何累积、强化或反转可能揭示最终概率所掩盖的不确定性。

Method: 提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪逐层MLP更新的累积路径，将其输入稀疏线性探针进行分类。

Result: 在选择性弃权任务中，该探针优于MSP，性能提升与基线校准误差呈正相关，最高达21个AURC点。

Conclusion: 每个特征具有闭合形式的几何含义，探针系数可追溯错误沿深度形成的过程：哪些层过早下结论、哪些层与当前状态矛盾、轨迹在何处偏离终点。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [120] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense是一种面向能量受限自主边缘系统的融合感知智能传感框架，通过三步训练流程（服务器端融合模型、过滤安全标签、边缘端紧凑融合模型）实现多模态数据的高效协同滤波，在RGB+深度/激光雷达双模态无人机数据集上取得高达33倍能耗降低（1%感兴趣目标出现率）和92.3%质量损失降低的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘系统多模态传感处理面临两难困境：要么在强大服务器端融合多模态数据（能耗高、延迟大），要么在近传感器端对单模态进行独立滤波（忽略跨模态依赖，导致冗余传输或漏检）。亟需一种融合感知的轻量级近传感器滤波方案，在降低计算和通信开销的同时保持任务质量。

Method: 提出三步训练流程：(i) 在服务器端训练融合模型学习下游任务；(ii) 生成过滤安全标签，量化各模态相对于融合决策的必要性；(iii) 将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入，压缩得到边缘端紧凑融合模型。运行时决策层联合降低计算与通信开销，且复杂度随传感器数量线性扩展。

Result: 在双模态（RGB+深度/激光雷达）SynDrone数据集上：1%感兴趣目标出现率时能耗降低33倍，10%时降低11倍；在固定30%数据缩减率下质量损失降低92.3%；能耗节省比最优基线高约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense通过融合感知的轻量级滤波机制，有效突破了单模态滤波忽略跨模态依赖的局限，在能量受限的自主边缘系统中实现了能耗、数据缩减与任务质量之间的显著平衡。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [121] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FuRA（全秩适应）方法，通过块张量-列车分解W=LSR，固定预训练SVD基的大核心L，仅优化紧凑核心R和块奇异值S，实现全秩谱预条件优化，在LLM微调、数学推理强化学习和视觉指令调优等任务中一致优于全微调，4位量化变体QFuRA也超越QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全微调和LoRA等参数高效微调方法在更新权重时未考虑预训练建立的谱结构，导致有限微调数据的噪声梯度会扰动鲁棒的预训练特征，缺乏谱预条件机制。

Method: 提出FuRA框架，基于块张量-列车分解W=LSR，固定预训练块SVD基的大核心L，仅优化紧凑核心R和块奇异值S，实现全秩谱预条件、全秩更新表达力，同时参数效率与LoRA相当。

Result: LLaMA-3-8B常识推理提升1.37，在LLM数学推理强化学习和视觉语言模型视觉指令调优中一致优于全微调；4位量化QFuRA超越QLoRA。

Conclusion: FuRA通过谱预条件设计有效约束更新到预训练列空间，在保持全秩表达能力的同时实现参数高效，是比全微调和LoRA更优的微调方案。

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [122] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MedExpMem经验记忆框架，使医学视觉语言模型能够像医生一样从诊断失败中积累鉴别诊断经验，在11个放射学子专科基准上实现最高7.0%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学视觉语言模型（VLM）参数编码的是静态知识，无法像经验丰富的医生一样在临床实践中通过病例积累鉴别诊断经验，缺乏区分易混淆疾病的能力。

Method: 提出MedExpMem框架，采用两阶段构建过程：初始实践暴露知识缺口，反思性再诊断完善理解。将诊断失败经验组织为成对鉴别笔记，编码关键鉴别因子、可操作决策规则和推理错误模式。遇到新病例时检索经验记忆指导鉴别推理。

Result: 在涵盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上，在不同模型和规模下均实现一致性准确率提升，最高提升7.0%。分析实验验证了经验质量和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedExpMem是一种有竞争力的方法，能够解决参数化学习无法触及的医学适应需求，使VLM具备动态积累鉴别诊断经验的能力。

Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

</details>


### [123] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵动态的推理流形（EDRM），通过早期解码熵信号自适应选择推理策略，在15个基准和4个LLM上实现41-55%的token缩减并提升准确率，揭示了推理应选择性调用而非默认使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证发现CoT在事实性任务和开放性任务上收益甚微甚至为负，同时消耗大量token，因此需要探究何时显式推理真正有益，并设计自适应推理策略。

Method: 通过分析早期解码阶段的熵动态，发现受益于CoT的任务呈现一致的熵下降模式，反之则不稳定或上升。基于此，提出EDRM框架，将熵轨迹嵌入紧凑可解释的流形表示，实现零样本部署和实例级自适应推理策略选择。

Result: 在15个基准和4个LLM上，EDRM在数据集级实现41-55%的token缩减并提升准确率（仅需50个校准样本）；在实例级进一步提升准确率达4.7%，同时节省27-45%的token。

Conclusion: 推理应选择性调用而非默认使用，熵驱动的解码控制是高效自适应LLM推理的有效方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the default strategy for enhancing LLM capabilities, yet its application raises a fundamental question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial? Empirical evidence reveals a striking paradox: CoT often provides marginal or even negative gains on factual and open-ended tasks while multiplying token consumption. In this work, we show that LLM reasoning is not a static property of tasks or models, but a \emph{dynamic decoding state} that emerges during generation. Through systematic analysis, we find early-stage entropy dynamics provide a reliable signal of this state: tasks benefiting from CoT exhibit consistent entropy reduction, while others display unstable or increasing patterns. This behavior can be interpreted as a phase-transition-like shift from a high-entropy exploratory regime to a low-entropy structured reasoning regime. Based on these insights, we propose \textbf{EDRM} (Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold), a lightweight and training-free routing framework that leverages early decoding entropy to adaptively select inference strategies. EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact and interpretable manifold representation, enabling both zero-shot deployment and fine-grained instance-level adaptation. Across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs of varying scales and architectures, EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines. At the dataset level, EDRM achieves \textbf{41--55\%} token reduction while improving accuracy with as few as 50 calibration samples. At the instance level, it further improves accuracy by up to \textbf{4.7\%} while maintaining \textbf{27--45\%} token savings. These results suggest that reasoning should be invoked selectively rather than by default, and demonstrate the effectiveness of entropy-driven decoding control for efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

</details>


### [124] [WeCon: An Efficient Weight-Conditioned Neural Solver for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22876)
*Xuan Wu,Jinbiao Chen,Yang Li,Lijie Wen,Chunguo Wu,Yuanshu Li,Yubin Xiao,Chunyan Miao,You Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种权重条件神经求解器WeCon，通过门控残差融合模块增强编码器中的权重-特征交互，采用残差融合模块缓解解码器中的权重信号稀释，并设计了高效偏好优化(EPO)方法生成高质量解对提升训练效果。在四个MOCOP变体上达到与POCCO-W相当的HyperVolume值，推理时间减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经求解器存在两个主要局限：(1) 权重注入方式不佳——要么仅在解码时注入一次导致权重条件上下文建模受限，要么主要在编码时注入导致解码过程中权重信号被稀释；(2) 偏好优化方法依赖纯随机采样构建解对，产生信息量不足的样本对，导致训练效率低下。

Method: (1) 编码器：设计包含三个注意力块和门控残差融合(GRF)块的编码器层，促进实例特征与权重的和谐交互，生成信息丰富的权重条件上下文；(2) 解码器：引入即插即用的残差融合(RF)模块，缓解权重信号稀释；(3) 训练：提出高效偏好优化(EPO)方法，构建高质量解对以生成更具信息量的配对样本，提升训练效果。

Result: 在四个MOCOP变体、不同问题规模和分布模式上，WeCon达到与SOTA求解器POCCO-W相当的HyperVolume值，同时推理时间减少40%。消融实验验证了所有设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: WeCon通过改进权重与特征在编解码器中的交互方式以及优化训练样本构建策略，有效解决了现有神经MOCOP求解器中权重条件建模和训练效率低下的问题，在保持求解质量的同时显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Existing neural solvers for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems (MOCOPs) commonly adopt decomposition-based strategies that scalarize an MOCOP into multiple subproblems associated with distinct weight vectors. However, they either inject weights only once during decoding, limiting weight-conditioned context modeling, or primarily during encoding, causing weight-signal dilution during decoding. Moreover, preference optimization methods rely on purely random sampling to construct solution pairs for training solvers, which often produces less informative pairs and thus leads to low training effectiveness. To better address these limitations, we propose an efficient Weight-Conditioned neural solver (WeCon). Specifically, we design an encoder layer with three attention blocks and our proposed Gated Residual Fusion (GRF) block to facilitate harmonious interaction between instance features and weights, thereby generating informative weight-conditioned context. We further introduce a plug-and-play Residual Fusion (RF) block in the decoder to alleviate weight-signal dilution. Finally, we propose Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO), which constructs high-quality solutions, thereby generating more informative pairs to improve training effectiveness. Experiments on four MOCOP variants across different problem scales and distribution patterns demonstrate that WeCon achieves HyperVolume (HV) values comparable to SOTA solver POCCO-W, while reducing inference time by 40%. Ablation studies validate the contributions of all designs.

</details>


### [125] [Tensor Cache: Eviction-conditioned Associative Memory for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22884)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tensor Cache提出一种两层缓存架构：L1使用滑动窗口softmax注意力保留近期精确注意力，L2将窗口逐出的KV对压缩为外积快速权重矩阵，并通过线性注意力恒等式实现高效读取。同时指出常用的分块均值训练捷径会引入虚假跨token外积，并提出加权求和扫描方法修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer的KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，滑动窗口缓存虽能约束内存但会完全丢弃被逐出的token，导致窗口外的相关证据无法访问。需要在保持内存效率的同时保留更长的上下文信息。

Method: 1) 两层缓存架构：L1为滑动窗口softmax注意力，L2为外积快速权重矩阵；2) 逐出的KV对被压缩为每层矩阵A，未来查询通过单次矩阵乘法读取；3) 可学习的标量门控融合L1和L2输出；4) 每头的衰减率和写入率参数端到端训练；5) 指出分块均值训练捷径会引入C²-C个虚假跨token外积，提出并行加权求和扫描替代。

Result: 在系统规模扩展、受控联想召回、长上下文语言建模和记忆容量诊断等实验中，Tensor Cache在内存-质量前沿上优于有界状态基线方法。

Conclusion: 外积记忆和读取恒等式是已知技术，本工作的贡献在于将其用作专门接收滑动窗口逐出内容的L2缓存，并识别和修正了分块均值训练中的虚假外积问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformer KV caches grow linearly with context length; sliding-window caching bounds memory but discards evicted tokens entirely, so relevant evidence outside the window becomes inaccessible. We introduce \emph{Tensor Cache}, a two-level cache that pairs sliding-window softmax attention as a first-level cache (L1) with a fixed-size outer-product fast-weight memory as a second-level cache (L2) fed by KV pairs evicted from the window. Recent tokens remain in exact local attention; evicted pairs are compressed into a per-layer matrix $A$ and read by future queries through a single matrix multiplication, exploiting the linear-attention identity $q_t(k_i \otimes v_i)=\langle q_t,k_i\rangle v_i$. A learned scalar gate fuses the L1 and L2 outputs, and per-head decay and write-rate parameters are trained end-to-end. The outer-product memory and the read identity are well-known; our contribution is their use as an L2 cache fed exclusively by sliding-window evictions, plus identifying that the common chunked-mean training shortcut $A\!\leftarrow\!λA\!+\!η(\bar k\!\otimes\!\bar v)$ silently introduces $C^2{-}C$ spurious cross-token outer products per chunk, and closing the gap with a parallel weighted-sum scan equivalent to per-token writes within float32 epsilon. Across systems scaling, controlled associative recall, long-context language modeling, and memory-capacity diagnostics, Tensor Cache improves the memory--quality frontier over bounded-state baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Pointwise Metrics Mislead: An Evaluation Protocol for Multimodal Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22891)
*Mads H. Baattrup,Jörn Bach,Laurids Jeppe,Finn Labe,Alexander Grohsjean,Christian Schwanenberger,Peer Stelldinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在科学重建逆向问题中，基于均方根误差/平均绝对误差的点式评价指标存在根本性缺陷——当后验分布为多峰时，点估计器会系统性压缩边际谱（变窄、丢失尾部、模态和形状），且该偏差与模型架构、训练规模和数据集大小无关。作者提出三部分评估协议：基于CRPS的逐事件分布精度、基于谱保真度的总体边际精度、基于覆盖校准的不确定性可信度。实验表明点式与分布度量下的模型排名完全相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并解决科学重建中广泛使用的点式评价指标（RMSE/MAE等）在多模态后验逆问题中的结构性失败问题，指出这些指标会导致系统性的谱压缩偏差，从而误导对模型优劣的判断和下游科学结论。

Method: 利用全方差定律从理论上证明点估计器在多模态后验下的边际谱变窄问题；提出三部分评估协议：(1) CRPS评估逐事件分布精度，(2) 频谱保真度诊断评估总体边际精度，(3) 覆盖校准评估不确定性可信度；在解析后验的合成基准和粒子物理学多对一逆问题实验上验证。

Result: 点式与分布度量下模型排名完全相反；CRPS无法区分的架构在校准步骤中被进一步分离；评估协议本身而非模型决定了科学结论。

Conclusion: 科学重建中必须摒弃仅依赖点式指标的评价惯例，应采用涵盖分布精度、边际精度和校准可信度的多维评估协议，否则会得出错误的科学结论。

Abstract: Evaluation in scientific reconstruction is dominated by pointwise metrics - RMSE, MAE, per-event resolution - under the implicit assumption that lower error means better reconstruction. We show that this assumption fails structurally for inverse problems with multimodal posteriors. By the law of total variance, point estimators trained to minimize MSE or MAE produce a marginal spectrum strictly narrower than the truth whenever the posterior has nonzero width. The resulting bias is independent of architecture, training, and dataset size, and it compresses precisely the spectral features - tails, modes, shapes - that downstream scientific measurements rely on. We propose a three-part evaluation protocol where each step targets a failure mode the others miss: per-event distributional accuracy via CRPS, population-level marginal accuracy via a spectrum-fidelity diagnostic, and uncertainty trustworthiness via coverage-based calibration. On a synthetic benchmark with an analytic posterior and on a realistic many-to-one inverse problem from particle physics, model rankings reverse between pointwise and distributional metrics, and calibration further separates architectures indistinguishable under CRPS. The evaluation protocol, not the model, determines the scientific conclusion.

</details>


### [127] [FIRMA: FIbonacci Ring Model Aggregation for Privacy-preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22898)
*Rachid Hedjam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRMA系列协议（\fibfl、\fibflp、\fibflpp），解决了联邦学习中的结构三元悖论：通过斐波那契加权环聚合、精度门控邻居抑制和2-opt环排列等技术，实现了无服务器、永久私有分类头、环拓扑和非对称邻居加权。\fibflpp在28个配置中超越FedAvg，在标签倾斜场景下最高领先+20.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议面临结构三元悖论：基于服务器的聚合存在单点故障和梯度反演风险；去中心化环状协议因均匀权重导致分类头暴露；个性化方法又重引入中心聚合。尚无协议能同时实现无服务器、永久私有分类头、环拓扑和原理性非对称邻居加权。

Method: 提出FIRMA系列协议，包含三个渐进增强版本：1) \fibfl：基于斐波那契加权的环聚合，分类头永久私有；2) \fibflp：引入精度门控邻居抑制，选择性降低欠收敛邻居权重；3) \fibflpp：增加2-opt环排列最大化相邻客户端类多样性、全局环覆盖（Kg=⌈N/2⌉轮gossip传递）和余弦退火自我保留校准。

Result: 在28个配置（4个基准×7种异构性）的系统实验中，\fibflpp在所有12个标签倾斜配置中超越FedAvg，CIFAR-10上K=1时最高领先+20.7个百分点。在Dirichlet异构性下，\fibflpp是无服务器协议中的Pareto最优方法，在28个配置中的17个达到最高准确率。

Conclusion: FIRMA系列协议成功解决了联邦学习的结构三元悖论，通过理论收敛率界和三项支撑命题证明了归一化、覆盖、保留和多样性的最优性。\fibflpp在标签倾斜场景下表现尤为突出，为去中心化联邦学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated learning protocols face a structural trilemma: canonical server-based aggregation~\cite{mcmahan2017} creates a single point of failure and gradient inversion risk; decentralised ring-gossip alternatives~\cite{hu2019segmented} expose classification heads to semi-honest peers via uninformed uniform weights; and personalised methods~\cite{collins2021exploiting} reintroduce central aggregation. No existing protocol simultaneously achieves server-free operation, permanently private heads, ring topology, and principled asymmetric neighbour weighting. We propose FIRMA (\textbf{FI}bonacci \textbf{R}ing \textbf{M}odel \textbf{A}ggregation), a family of three progressively enhanced federated learning protocols: 1) \fibfl\ establishes the foundation: server-free ring aggregation with Fibonacci-weighted neighbour blending and permanently private classification heads. 2) \fibflp\ augments this with accuracy-gated neighbour suppression, selectively down-weighting poorly-converged peers while preserving the Fibonacci directional bias. 3) \fibflpp, the full system, completes the family with a 2-opt ring permutation that maximises adjacent-client class diversity, global ring coverage via $K_g{=}\lceil N/2\rceil$ gossip passes, and cosine-annealed self-retention calibration. We establish a convergence rate bound and three supporting propositions governing normalisation, coverage, retention, and diversity optimality. Systematic experiments across 28 configurations -- four benchmarks crossed with seven heterogeneity regimes -- demonstrate that \fibflpp\ surpasses \fedavg\ in all 12 label-skew configurations, with a peak advantage of $+20.7$\,pp on CIFAR-10 at $K{=}1$. Under Dirichlet heterogeneity, \fibflpp\ is the Pareto-dominant method among all server-free protocols, achieving the highest accuracy in 17 of 28 configurations.

</details>


### [128] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段联邦推荐系统流程，将非敏感的用户偏好数据与从不离开设备的敏感移动上下文数据分离：第一阶段在云端基于非敏感数据运行协同过滤生成候选列表，第二阶段在设备端利用敏感信号进行重排序，仅梯度/模型更新离开设备。在多个数据集上验证，并提供了跨平台生产级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化推荐需要将敏感用户数据集中到服务器，这违背了现代隐私期望和地域法规；移动设备上既要实现个性化内容推荐，又要保护用户敏感的移动上下文数据（如位置、活动状态等）不外泄。

Method: 两阶段联邦推荐流水线：(1) 云端阶段——基于非敏感的应用上下文数据运行协同过滤模型，生成候选物品短列表；(2) 设备端阶段——利用敏感的移动信号（如传感器数据）对候选列表进行重排序，仅模型更新/梯度离开设备，原始数据始终驻留设备端。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI Human Activity Recognition以及一个专有试点数据集上验证了方法有效性，并交付了支持Android和iOS的Kotlin Multiplatform生产级库。

Conclusion: 通过原则性的数据分离设计和联邦学习机制，可在保护敏感移动上下文数据的前提下实现高效的个性化推荐，并具备跨平台生产部署能力。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [129] [MARGIN: Runtime Confidence Calibration for Multi-Agent Foundation Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22949)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARGIN是一种无需模型访问、无需保留数据、无需重训练的在线校准方法，通过对称指数加权移动平均与贝叶斯收缩混合，在任务流中学习每个智能体在每个置信度区间的校准因子。在19个基础模型、8个基准测试、5万+观测上，分布漂移下校准误差比最优设计时基线低3-6倍；多智能体选择中将成对分辨率从低于随机的45-56%提升至70-89%，在四个基准中的三个上超越始终最佳模型预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体在多智能体部署中，协调者需要判断信任哪个智能体的响应。标准方法按智能体自报告的置信度加权，但基础模型置信度存在系统性校准偏差，且在困难任务上与准确率呈负相关。设计时校准方法（温度缩放、Platt缩放、直方图分箱）因对保留数据拟合固定修正且在分布漂移下退化而无法解决此问题。

Method: MARGIN（Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization）是一种在线校准方法：从任务流本身学习每个智能体在每个置信度区间的校准因子，无需模型访问、保留数据或重训练。核心机制为对称指数加权移动平均与贝叶斯收缩混合，仅需三个超参数且具有鲁棒默认值。

Result: 在19个基础模型、8个基准、5万+观测上：分布漂移下校准误差比最优设计时基线低3-6倍；原始语言化置信度在困难基准上的成对分辨率低于随机（45-56%），MARGIN将其完全校正至70-89%，并在四个基准中的三个上超越始终最佳模型预言机。六项形式化命题刻画了收敛性、跟踪速度及非策略智能体对称更新的最优性，所有预测均经实证验证。

Conclusion: MARGIN通过在线校准有效解决了基础模型置信度在分布漂移下的系统性偏差问题，显著提升了多智能体选择中协调决策的可靠性，且无需模型内部访问或额外数据收集。

Abstract: Foundation model agents increasingly operate in multi-agent deployments where a coordinator must decide which agent's response to trust. The standard approach weights agents by their self-reported confidence, but recent evidence shows that foundation model confidence is systematically mis-calibrated and, on hard tasks, inversely correlated with accuracy. Design-time calibration methods (temperature scaling, Platt scaling, histogram binning) cannot address this problem because they fit a fixed correction to held-out data and degrade under distribution shift.
  We present MARGIN (Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization), an online calibration method that learns per-agent, per-confidence-band calibration factors from the task stream itself, requiring no model access, no held-out data, and no retraining. MARGIN uses symmetric exponentially weighted moving averages with Bayesian shrinkage blending, and has three hyperparameters with robust defaults. Across 19 foundation models, 8 benchmarks, and over 50,000 observations, MARGIN achieves 3-6x lower calibration error than the best design-time baseline under distribution shift. In multi-agent selection, raw verbalized confidence produces pairwise resolution worse than random (45-56%) on hard benchmarks. MARGIN corrects this completely, raising pairwise resolution to 70-89% and surpassing the always-best-model oracle on three of four benchmarks. Six formal propositions characterize convergence, tracking speed, and the optimality of symmetric updates for non-strategic agents, with all predictions illustrated empirically.

</details>


### [130] [FederatedRSF : Federated Random Survival Forests for Partially Overlapping Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22954)
*Maryam Moradpour,Jonas Harriehausen,Amirreza Aleyasin,Lion Philipp Wolf,Youngjun Park,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FederatedRSF，一个基于联邦随机生存森林的Python包，能够在特征部分重叠的多中心场景下，无需共享原始数据即可进行生存预测，性能与集中式训练相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心生存预测需要跨机构数据以提高鲁棒性和泛化性，但隐私法规和机构治理禁止共享患者级临床和基因组数据，且不同机构的特征集仅部分重叠，现有方法难以同时满足隐私保护和特征异质性处理的需求。

Method: FederatedRSF实现了联邦随机生存森林，通过聚合本地训练的生存树，仅将特征兼容的树分发给各站点，在特征部分重叠的情况下实现推理，无需共享原始数据。

Result: 在GBSG2乳腺癌队列上模拟特征异质性进行实验，使用Harrell's C-Index评估，联邦模型达到了与集中式训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: FederatedRSF在保护隐私和应对特征异质性的同时，实现了与集中式训练可比的预测性能，为多中心生存预测提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Multi-center survival prediction can improve robustness and generalizability, yet privacy regulations and institutional governance often prevent pooling patient-level clinical and genomic data across institutions. In practice, deployment is further complicated by feature-space heterogeneity, in which sites collect different covariates or use different sequencing panels, resulting in only partially overlapping feature sets. We present FederatedRSF, a Python package that implements federated random survival forests, aggregating locally trained survival trees and redistributing only feature-compatible trees to each site, enabling inference with partial overlap without sharing raw data. We evaluate FederatedRSF on the GBSG2 breast cancer cohort distributed with the scikit-survival package, simulating feature heterogeneity across clients by withholding subsets of features, and assessing discrimination using Harrell's concordance index (C-Index) under repeated cross-validation and site-splits. The results demonstrated that the federated model can achieve performance comparable to that of the centralized training setting.

</details>


### [131] [Learned Relay Representations for Forward-Thinking Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22967)
*Benjamin Rozonoyer,Jacopo Minniti,Dhruvesh Patel,Neil Band,Avishek Joey Bose,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Learned Relay Representations (Relay)方法，让掩码扩散模型在去噪步骤间传递潜在信息，避免内部计算被丢弃，在保持或提升性能的同时将推理延迟降低32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDMs）在迭代生成序列时，每一步去噪后会丢弃前一步的内部计算信息（模型表示），导致后续步骤必须重新计算这些有价值的信息，造成计算浪费。

Method: 提出Relay，引入一个可微分的逐token通道，在前向传播间传递潜在信息；通过截断式时间反向传播（BPTT）训练，并与块扩散和KV缓存技术兼容。

Result: 在Sudoku规划任务上验证了设计选择的合理性；在Fast-dLLM v2（SOTA扩散语言模型）上，在编码任务中超越标准监督微调，同时推理延迟降低32%。

Conclusion: SOTA扩散语言模型可以通过显式训练在解码步骤间中继传递潜在信息，推进了性能-延迟帕累托前沿。

Abstract: When Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) generate sequences through iterative refinement, the rich internal computation over masked positions is discarded, forcing every subsequent refinement step to recompute the valuable internal information stored as model representations. To avoid a hard reset between denoising rounds, we propose Learned Relay Representations (Relay), a method that allows MDMs to be forward-thinking when denoising by explicitly learning how to propagate latent information for the benefit of future denoising steps. Relay introduces a differentiable per-token channel that passes information between forward passes and is trained via truncated backpropagation through time (BPTT). We show that this framework can be scaled to state-of-the-art Diffusion Language Models (DLMs), and is seamlessly compatible with techniques like block diffusion and KV caching. We first provide a thorough justification of the design choices in Relay on a challenging Sudoku-based planning task. We then scale Relay to Fast-dLLM v2, a state-of-the-art DLM, outperforming standard supervised finetuning on coding tasks while reducing inference latency by up to 32%. Our empirical results demonstrate that state-of-the-art DLMs can be explicitly trained to relay latent information forward across decoding steps, advancing the performance-latency Pareto frontier. We provide code for all our experiments.

</details>


### [132] [A mathematical theory of balancing relational generalization and memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22972)
*Luke Cheng,Samuel Lippl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项名为“带例外的传递推理”的新任务，用于研究学习系统如何在关系泛化与例外记忆之间取得平衡。通过核岭回归模型的理论分析和预训练语言模型的实验验证，揭示了表示几何对成功泛化的关键影响，并指出了现有模型的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏合适的任务范式来研究学习系统如何在一般规律（泛化）与特殊例外（记忆）之间取得平衡，这一能力对于人类、动物和现代机器学习模型的智能行为至关重要。

Method: 1. 提出新任务：传递推理带例外（transitive inference with exceptions），测试关系泛化与例外记忆的能力。2. 使用核岭回归（kernel ridge regression）进行理论分析，覆盖广泛的表示族和任务参数。3. 在预训练语言模型上进行微调验证。

Result: 1. 学习系统能够在关系泛化与例外记忆之间取得平衡。2. 与无例外的传递推理不同，成功泛化对表示几何（representational geometry）高度敏感。3. 预训练语言模型能按传递规则泛化，但会做出理论预测的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了学习系统平衡关系泛化与例外记忆的机制，解释了泛化失败的原因，并强调了设计新任务范式以探索这一能力的重要性。

Abstract: Humans, animals, and modern machine learning models exhibit impressive abilities to learn complex behaviors and generalize these behaviors to unseen situations. This ability requires us to learn rules and regularities that allow for such generalizations. At the same time, in most complex environments, any rule will have its exceptions. How do learning systems balance between learning general regularities and memorizing exceptions? We argue that a lack of task paradigms has hindered the study of this essential ability. To address this gap, we introduce a novel task, transitive inference with exceptions, that tests for relational generalization and memorization of an exception to the relational rule. We then analytically characterize the behavior of a simple, theoretically tractable model of neural network learning (kernel ridge regression) across a broad family of representations and task parameters. We find that these models can balance between relational generalization and memorization, but unlike for transitive inference without an exception, successful generalization is sensitive to the specific representational geometry. We explain why this task is more challenging mechanistically by drawing on our analytical theory. Finally, we validate our theoretical insights in pretrained language models that are finetuned on ordered relations, finding that these models successfully generalize according to the transitive rule, but also make the kinds of systematic mistakes predicted by our theory. Overall, our theory shows how learning systems can balance between relational generalization and memorization, explains how this can go wrong, and emphasizes the need for new task paradigms designed to probe this ability.

</details>


### [133] [Worse than Random: The Importance of a Baseline for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22973)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Michael E. Houle,Oussama Chelly,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将随机特征选择作为无监督特征选择方法的评估基线，实证表明许多现有最先进方法在性能和效率上均不如随机选择，因此强调新方法开发中必须与随机基线进行比较以确保实质性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无监督特征选择方法缺乏统一的评估基线，难以判断新方法相较于现有文献的实际价值增量。

Method: 提出将随机特征选择作为无监督特征选择方法的评估基线，并通过实验对比多种现有方法与随机选择的性能和效率。

Result: 许多最先进的无监督特征选择方法在性能和效率上均被随机特征选择所超越。

Conclusion: 强调在开发新的无监督特征选择方法时，必须将随机特征选择作为基线进行严格比较，以确保方法能带来实质性改进。

Abstract: Many novel unsupervised feature selection methods are proposed each year, yet their empirical evaluation is limited to supervised and unsupervised evaluation metrics computed on selected datasets, along with comparisons to existing methods. However, in the absence of an established evaluation baseline, it is difficult to determine the value added to the existing literature by each of these methods, and how effective their underlying approaches are. We propose using random feature selection as a baseline for evaluating the unsupervised feature selection methods. We empirically show that many of the state-of-the-art methods in unsupervised feature selection are outperformed by random feature selection in both performance and efficiency. Accordingly, we emphasize on the strict requirement of considering random feature selection as a baseline in the development process of novel unsupervised feature selection methods to ensure a consistent improvement over random feature selection.

</details>


### [134] [Test-Time Training Undermines Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22984)
*Simone Antonelli,Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了测试时训练（TTT）这一新兴范式存在严重安全漏洞，攻击者可利用其三种威胁模型绕过安全过滤器，在LoRA下少样本和生成阶段威胁模型平均ASR@10分别达到95%和93%，且该漏洞可迁移至生产微调API。作者提出了基于困惑度偏移的轻量级检测器作为防御初步方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT作为一种在推理阶段动态调整模型参数的新范式，虽然能提升少样本学习、检索增强生成和复杂推理等任务性能，但其动态适应性引入了新的安全漏洞，可能被攻击者利用来越狱模型。目前缺乏对TTT安全风险的系统性研究。

Method: 作者首先识别了TTT的三种威胁模型（少样本、生成阶段等），然后通过实验展示攻击者如何利用这些威胁模型绕过安全过滤器。采用ASR和ASR@10作为评估指标，在多个不同系列和规模的模型上进行测试。针对TTT诱导的过拟合导致标准评估指标虚高的问题，提出了有效性感知的评估方法进行修正。最后提出基于私有有害保留集上困惑度偏移的轻量级服务端检测器作为防御手段。

Result: 在LoRA设置下，少样本威胁模型和生成阶段威胁模型在不同系列和规模模型上的平均ASR@10分别达到95%和93%。这些漏洞可迁移至生产微调API。TTT诱导的过拟合会产生退化输出，导致标准评估指标下的ASR被虚高。

Conclusion: TTT暴露了新的攻击面，增强了攻击能力，并破坏了现有的安全防护措施。作者提出的基于困惑度偏移的轻量级检测器可作为初步防御，但稳健部署最终需要动态对齐方法。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) is an emerging paradigm that enables models to adapt their parameters during inference, improving performance on tasks such as few-shot learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and complex reasoning. However, this dynamic adaptation introduces new vulnerabilities that adversaries can exploit to jailbreak models. We identify three threat models for TTT and demonstrate how attackers can leverage them to bypass safety filters. Our results show that TTT can significantly increase the Attack Success Rate (ASR) and the ASR over 10 generation trials (ASR@10). For example, under LoRA, the few-shot and generation-phase threat models achieve an average ASR@10 of 95% and 93% respectively, across models from different families and scales. These vulnerabilities transfer to production fine-tuning APIs. We also show that TTT-induced overfitting can produce degenerate outputs that inflate ASR under standard judges, and propose a validity-aware evaluation to correct for this. Our findings suggest that TTT exposes a new attack surface, strengthens attacks, and undermines existing safety guardrails. As a first step toward defense, we propose a lightweight provider-side detector that flags TTT requests via the perplexity shift on a private harmful holdout, but robust deployment will ultimately require dynamic alignment.

</details>


### [135] [Smoothed Elicitation Complexity for Approximate $Γ$-calibration of Discrete Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23017)
*Jessica Finocchiaro,Victor Ganson,Drona Khurana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散属性的近似校准问题，通过Lipschitz连续属性作为中介，首次为强可排序的离散属性提供了近似校准结果，并刻画了其Lipschitz elicitation复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类校准面临指数级复杂度爆炸问题，Noarov和Roth (2023)提出的属性校准将复杂度从类别数n降至属性维度d（elicitation复杂度）。但现有近似属性校准工作局限于连续标量属性，而许多重要属性（如众数、排序）是离散的，缺乏近似校准的理论保证。

Method: 利用Lipschitz连续属性作为中介桥梁，将强可排序的离散属性通过构造Lipschitz属性进行近似校准，再通过后处理恢复原始离散属性。同时刻画了强可排序离散属性的Lipschitz elicitation复杂度。

Result: 首次为离散属性提供了近似校准的理论结果，特别针对强可排序的离散属性。证明了通过Lipschitz属性中介可以构造可后处理恢复原离散属性的算法。

Conclusion: 将近似属性校准从连续标量属性拓展到了离散属性，为多类校准中离散属性（如众数、排序）的可靠性评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: One prominent method of evaluating machine learning model trustworthiness is the notion of calibration. In the binary outcome setting, a probabilistic predictor is calibrated if outcomes are realized according to a model's distributional prediction, conditioned on this prediction. Straightforward extensions of binary calibration definitions to probabilistic multiclass classifiers suffer from an exponential complexity blowup as the space of predictions grows exponentially in the number of classes $n$. As a remedy, Noarov and Roth (2023) propose multiclass calibration with predictions that are properties of the outcome distribution, reducing complexity from growing in the number of classes $n$ to the dimension $d$ of the property, called its elicitation complexity. Previous work on approximate property calibration is generally limited to continuous scalar properties, despite many relevant properties of interest being discrete, like the mode or rankings. We characterize the approximate property calibration of discrete properties which are strongly orderable by using Lipschitz continuous properties as an intermediary. This work is the first to our knowledge to provide approximate calibration results for discrete properties. Along the way, we characterize the Lipschitz elicitation complexity of strongly orderable discrete properties by constructing algorithms for designing these Lipschitz properties, which we prove can be post-processed to obtain the original discrete property.

</details>
