### [136] [World Machine: Towards Generative World Modeling for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23025)
*Elton Cardoso do Nascimento,Alexandre da Silva Simões,Esther Luna Colombini,Ricardo Ribeiro Gudwin,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出World Machine，一种基于Transformer的生成式世界建模架构，通过引入潜在状态实现对时间序列的高效预测和可控模拟，在合成数据集Toy1D上验证了其相比传统Transformer在计算效率和上下文适应性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在处理时间序列时，计算和存储代价随上下文长度呈二次方增长，且难以适应不同长度的观测数据。世界模型作为一种新兴范式，希望通过结构化、可泛化的方式实现对环境的预测性理解和可控模拟，但现有方法在效率上存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出World Machine架构，基于Transformer并引入潜在状态（latent states），使模型能够适应不同数量的观测数据和上下文长度，从而克服传统Transformer的二次方计算复杂度问题。

Result: 在合成的Toy1D数据集上进行了实验验证，结果表明该方法的可行性，展现了传统Transformer不具备的能力，并验证了训练协议中各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: World Machine通过潜在状态机制有效提升了时间序列建模的计算效率和适应性，为世界模型在生成式AI中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World models represent a paradigm shift in generative AI, pursuing predictive understanding and controllable simulation of environments in a structured and generalizable way. We present World Machine, a generative world-modeling architecture for time series. It is a transformer-based architecture with latent states that enables adaptation to different amounts of observed data and contexts. This shows an improvement over traditional transformers, which have a computational and memory cost that scales quadratically with the context. Experiments on a proposed synthetic dataset, Toy1D, validate the approach's feasibility, demonstrate capabilities not found in conventional transformers, and highlight the contributions of each component of the training protocol.

</details>


### [137] [RADAR: Relative Angular Divergence Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23028)
*Xavier Cadet,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RADAR，一种基于几何的跨领域迁移性度量方法，通过分析基础模型中层间表征的轨迹（角度对齐、距离变化和分布差异）来评估跨域迁移效果，在多个视觉和文本基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在数据扩充时可能引发负迁移（性能下降），缺乏有效的跨域迁移性预估指标，RADAR旨在通过表征空间的几何属性解决这一问题。

Method: RADAR通过测量层到层的位移轨迹中的角度对齐和相对距离变化，比较域内与跨域动态的实证分布，基于轨迹分布之间的散度来估计迁移性。

Result: 在跨语言情感分类和跨域图像分类等多个场景中，RADAR在多个视觉和文本基准上提供了具有竞争力的预测性能，尤其在领域过渡平滑或清晰分离时表现突出。

Conclusion: 迁移性估计的有效性取决于模型内部表征空间的几何结构，不同模态偏好不同的拓扑形式；简单几何度量即可有效预测跨域迁移效果。

Abstract: Machine learning methods rely on data. However, gathering suitable data can be challenging due to availability constraints, cost, or the need for domain expertise. Expanding datasets with additional sources is a common response to limited data, yet this practice does not always improve downstream performance and can sometimes lead to a loss of performance, known as negative transfer. We propose RADAR, a simple, geometrically grounded metric for estimating cross-domain transferability in foundation models. RADAR analyzes the layer-wise evolution of representations by measuring angular alignments and relative changes in distance along layer-to-layer displacement trajectories, and by comparing empirical distributions of within-domain and cross-domain dynamics. We hypothesize that domain transferability is related to the divergence between these trajectory distributions. We evaluate the metric across multiple modalities, including cross-lingual sentiment classification with text embedding models and cross-domain image classification with foundation vision models. Across several settings, RADAR provides competitive predictive performance relative to existing transferability metrics on several vision and text benchmarks, with particularly strong results when domain transitions are smooth or cleanly separated. Our ablations further suggest that the effectiveness of transferability estimation depends on the geometry of the model's internal representation space, with different modalities favoring different topological formulations.

</details>


### [138] [Uncovering the Latent Potential of Deep Intermediate Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23033)
*Arnesh Batra,Arush Gumber,Aniket Khandelwal,Jashn Khemani,Anubha Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练模型中的任务相关信息在深度方向呈非单调分布，标准做法仅使用最终层或浅层混合会丢失信息。为此提出LOES（逐层最优嵌入选择）方法，通过正交和各向同性约束下的残差最小化识别任务判别性子空间，并引入GeoReg（几何正则化损失）在微调中维持类流形的单纯形结构。实验表明该方法在多种架构、深度、模态和数据规模下均优于基线，且深度越大提升越显著，同时支持跨语言和跨模态的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅使用最终层或浅层混合进行迁移学习，忽视了任务相关信息在模型各层中呈非单调分布的事实，导致信息丢失和次优迁移性能。

Method: 1) LOES：一种构造性谱方法，通过在正交性和各向同性约束下最小化残差误差来识别任务判别性子空间；2) GeoReg：几何正则化损失，在微调过程中强制类流形保持单纯形结构，稳定表示几何。

Result: 在多种架构、深度、模态和数据规模下，LOES持续优于标准基线，且性能增益随模型深度增加而增大；此外还揭示了语义因子在各层中的分布，实现了跨语言和跨模态的可解释性分析。

Conclusion: 层间嵌入几何结构并非偶然现象，而是深度模型表示和迁移知识的核心机制，本文的方法为此提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Foundational Models pretrained on huge amount of data learn representations that evolve across depth, forming a hierarchy of embeddings with distinct semantic content and geometric structure. Contrary to the widespread practice of using only the final layer or shallow mixtures, we show that task-relevant information is distributed non-monotonically across layers and cannot be recovered by naïve aggregation. Through a geometric and empirical study across multiple modalities, we show that effective transfer depends on identifying which layers encode task-discriminative structure and how their embeddings are geometrically organized. We introduce Layer-wise Optimal Embedding Selection (LOES), a constructive spectral method that identifies task-discriminative subspaces by minimizing residual error under orthogonality and isotropy constraints. To align fine-tuning with this selection principle, we further propose Geometric Regularization Loss (GeoReg), which enforces a simplicial structure on class manifolds and stabilizes representation geometry during fine-tuning. Across a wide range of architectures, depths, modalities, and data regimes, LOES consistently outperforms standard baselines, with gains that grow as model depth increases. Beyond accuracy, our method reveals how semantic factors are distributed across layers, thereby enabling cross-lingual and cross-modal interpretability analyses. Together, our results provide strong evidence that layerwise embedding geometry is not incidental but central to how deep models represent and transfer knowledge.

</details>


### [139] [Steered Generation via Gradient-Based Optimization on Sparse Query Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23040)
*Sumanta Bhattacharyya,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原型稀疏引导（Prototype-Based Sparse Steering）的方法，通过将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于注意力查询激活，实现对大语言模型生成过程的精确、可解释控制，并在逻辑规划（Gridworld）和教育文本风格（Bloom认知分类）两个领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在引导方法对密集状态进行干预时容易混淆不同的语义特征，缺乏精确性和可解释性。作者假设直接操纵注意力机制比笼统的状态干预能提供更锐利的可控性。

Method: 提出Prototype-Based Sparse Steering框架：1) 将SAE专门应用于注意力查询激活，分解为可解释的稀疏特征；2) 在推理阶段应用基于梯度的优化，使稀疏表示与目标行为的类原型对齐。

Result: 在Textualized Gridworld中，优化稀疏查询特征能有效满足刚性规划约束（如安全路径vs短路径）；在高维教育领域，框架能控制反馈的认知复杂度（基于Bloom认知分类）。

Conclusion: 稀疏查询表示提供了必要的解耦能力，能够实现对逻辑规划和风格细微差别的统一、可解释控制，验证了该方法的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Latent steering exploits internal representations of Large Language Models (LLMs) to guide generation, yet interventions on dense states can entangle distinct semantic features. In this paper, we investigate attention query activations as a high-fidelity site for precise control, hypothesizing that manipulating the attention mechanism itself offers sharper steerability than general state interventions. We introduce Prototype-Based Sparse Steering, a framework that applies Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) specifically to query activations, to decompose them into interpretable features, then apply gradient-based optimization during inference to align the sparse representation with class prototypes of target behaviors. To validate this architectural insight, we first analyze the mechanism in Textualized Gridworld, a controlled environment for verifiable planning constraints. We demonstrate that optimizing sparse query features enables effective navigation of rigid planning requirements (i.e., safe vs. short paths), confirming the method's ability to satisfy objective rules. We then demonstrate the framework's versatility by training SAEs on a high-dimensional educational domain, where the framework steers the cognitive complexity of feedback (i.e., Bloom's Taxonomy). Our experiments establish that sparse query representations provide the necessary disentanglement for unified, interpretable control over both logical planning and stylistic nuance.

</details>


### [140] [Decomposing and Measuring Evaluation Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23055)
*Changling Li,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Jie Zhang,Zhijing Jin,Sahar Abdelnabi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一个基于社会心理学的评估意识分析框架，将评估意识分解为环境组件（任务可识别性）和模型组件（识别与行为倾向），并通过EvalAwareBench基准（100个可控制8种触发因素的成对安全-能力任务）对9个前沿模型进行测试，发现识别率取决于模型与基准的特定组合，识别很少导致行为改变，模型对安全评估比对能力评估更敏感，且叠加因素会逐步提升所有模型的评估意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型有时会意识到自己正在被评估并调整行为，从而损害基准测试结果的有效性。然而现有研究缺乏共享基础，混淆了评估属性与模型属性，以及检测与行为响应。因此需要建立统一的理论框架来系统研究这一现象。

Method: 基于社会心理学将评估意识分解为环境组件（8种触发因素：占位实体、评分式输出格式等）和模型组件（识别与行为倾向），通过思维链监控研究识别和行为，并构建EvalAwareBench基准（100个可独立切换8种因素的成对安全-能力任务），对9个前沿模型在4个基准上进行测试。

Result: 识别率取决于模型与基准的特定组合而非孤立因素；识别很少导致行为改变，且改变方向取决于感知到的评估类型；模型对安全评估比能力评估更敏感；没有单一因素能统一影响所有模型，但叠加因素会逐步提升所有模型的评估意识。

Conclusion: 该框架和EvalAwareBench提供了测量、归因和缓解评估意识的工具，指出在识别下保持行为一致性是有前途的前进方向。

Abstract: Frontier language models sometimes recognize that they are being evaluated and adjust their behavior, undermining validity of benchmark results. Yet the field studies it without a shared foundation, conflating properties of the evaluation with properties of the model, and detection with behavioral response. We ground evaluation awareness in social psychology, decomposing it into an environment component (how recognizable the task is) and a model component that separates recognition from propensity to act on it. We operationalize the environment component through eight categorized trigger factors, such as placeholder entities and grading-style output formats, and study recognition and behavior through chain-of-thought monitoring. Across nine frontier models and four benchmarks, recognition rates depend on the specific pairing of model and benchmark rather than on either in isolation. Recognition rarely leads to behavioral change, and when it does, the direction depends on the type of evaluation perceived. Models are also more sensitive to safety than capability evaluations, placing safety benchmark validity at greater risk. To study which factors each model is sensitive to and how they interact, we propose \textbf{EvalAwareBench}, a factor-controlled benchmark of 100 paired safety-capability tasks where each of the eight factors can be independently toggled, varying evaluative signals while holding the underlying request fixed. Through EvalAwareBench, we find that no single factor uniformly affects all models, but stacking factors progressively raises evaluation awareness across all of them. Our framework and EvalAwareBench provide the tools to measure, attribute, and mitigate evaluation awareness, pointing to behavioral consistency under recognition as a promising path forward.

</details>


### [141] [ModeSwitch-LLM: A Lightweight Phase-Aware Controller for Cross-Mode LLM Inference on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23057)
*Aman Sunesh,Ali Alshehhi,Hivansh Dhakne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModeSwitch-LLM是一个轻量级请求边界控制器，通过为每个请求选择合适的推理模式（FP16、量化、推测解码等）来提升单GPU大模型推理效率，在Llama-3.1-8B上实现2.10倍延迟加速和51.7%能耗降低，且精度几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型推理服务通常采用单一的静态配置，无法为不同请求动态选择最优推理模式，导致效率低下。作者旨在通过请求级别的轻量路由控制，在不重新训练模型或改变架构的前提下提升单GPU推理效率。

Method: 提出ModeSwitch-LLM系统，包含一个轻量级的在线控制器，利用廉价的请求级工作负载特征，为每个请求从FP16、量化模式（GPTQ等）、推测解码、混合模式（如GPTQ+前缀缓存、INT8+连续批处理）中选择合适的固定推理模式。控制器采用基于规则的路由策略，并与轻量级学习路由方法进行对比。

Result: 在Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct单A100 GPU上，规则控制器实现2.10倍平均延迟加速（对比FP16）、0.48倍平均能耗比（每token能耗降低51.7%），精度仅+0.17个百分点偏差。学习路由方法因额外开销和违反约束问题未明显优于规则方法。

Conclusion: 简单的请求感知路由能够在无需重新训练模型或改变架构的情况下，从现有推理模式中恢复显著的效率收益，证明了请求级别动态模式选择的有效性。

Abstract: ModeSwitch-LLM is a lightweight request-boundary controller for improving single-GPU large language model inference efficiency by routing each request to an appropriate fixed inference mode. Instead of relying on one static serving configuration, the system selects among FP16, quantized modes, speculative decoding, and hybrid modes such as GPTQ plus prefix caching and INT8 plus continuous batching using cheap workload-level features. We evaluate ModeSwitch-LLM on Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct served on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU. On deployment-style synthetic workloads, the online controller achieves a 2.10x mean latency speedup over FP16 and a 0.48x mean energy ratio, corresponding to 51.7% lower energy per token. On automatic benchmarks used as a quality gate, accuracy remains close to FP16 with a mean delta of +0.17 percentage points. We also evaluate lightweight learned routers, but find that they do not clearly outperform the rule-based controller because they add routing overhead and more often select modes that violate quality, energy, or memory constraints. These results show that simple request-aware routing can recover substantial efficiency from existing inference modes without retraining the model or changing its architecture.

</details>


### [142] [GEMQ: Global Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23078)
*Jianing Deng,Song Wang,Dongwei Wang,Zijie Liu,Tianlong Chen,Huanrui Yang,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出全局专家级混合精度量化方法GEMQ，通过线性规划分配专家位宽和路由器微调适应量化，有效降低MoE-LLMs内存开销并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度量化方法依赖逐层重要性估计，且忽视了量化引起的路由器偏移，导致位宽分配和路由决策次优，无法充分发挥MoE-LLMs的压缩潜力。

Method: GEMQ包含两个核心组件：(1)基于量化误差分析的全局线性规划公式，用于捕获模型范围内专家重要性并分配位宽；(2)高效的路由器微调，使路由机制适应量化后的专家。两者集成到渐进式量化框架中，迭代优化重要性估计和位宽分配。

Result: 实验表明GEMQ显著降低内存占用并加速推理，同时保持极小的精度损失。

Conclusion: GEMQ通过全局专家级位宽分配和路由器自适应，突破了现有方法的局限性，在MoE-LLMs压缩中实现了更优的精度-内存权衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models (MoE-LLMs) achieve strong performance but incur substantial memory overhead due to massive expert parameters. Mixed-precision quantization mitigates this cost by allocating expert-wise bit-widths based on their importance, approaching the accuracy-memory Pareto frontier and enabling extreme low-bit quantization. However, existing methods rely on layer-wise importance estimation and overlook router shifts induced by quantization, resulting in suboptimal allocation and routing. In this work, we propose Global Expert-level Mixed-precision Quantization (GEMQ) to overcome these limitations via (1) a global linear-programming formulation that captures model-wide expert importance based on quantization error analysis, and (2) efficient router fine-tuning to adapt routing to quantized experts. These components are integrated into a progressive quantization framework that iteratively refines importance estimation and allocation. Experiments demonstrate that GEMQ significantly reduces memory and accelerates inference with minimal accuracy degradation. Source code is available at https://github.com/jndeng/GEMQ .

</details>


### [143] [The Attribution Contract: Feature Attribution for Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23080)
*Giang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出将分类器时代的特征归因方法直接迁移到生成式语言模型存在概念性局限，并提出了"归因契约"(Attribution Contract)规范来明确归因声明中的各项要素，主张特征归因方法应作为"方法-契约"配对进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语言模型中，何为"特征"存在歧义：自回归模型中已生成token既是输出又是后续输入，扩散语言模型通过迭代去噪/去掩码生成，局部解释可能指向扩散状态而非下一个token。这种歧义不仅是实现细节，更是将分类器时代的特征归因直接带入生成式语言建模的概念性局限。

Method: 提出"归因契约"(Attribution Contract)规范，明确命名被解释的输出、哪些特征有资格获得归因、假设的生成过程、固定不变的内容以及被归因的模型得分。以自回归语言模型和扩散语言模型为案例进行分析。

Result: 归因契约阐明了相同的归因方法在不同的实例化方式下可以回答不同的问题；许多关于生成式语言模型特征归因的争议并非源于归因算法本身，而是源于未言明的解释契约；展示了归因于早期生成token、中间状态或去噪阶段何时有信息量、何时具有误导性。

Conclusion: 生成式语言模型中的特征归因方法应作为"方法-契约"配对进行评估。

Abstract: Feature attribution methods promise to identify which input features matter for a model output. In generative language models, however, it is often unclear what should count as a feature in the first place. In autoregressive language models, earlier generated tokens are both outputs of the model and inputs to later predictions. In diffusion language models, generation proceeds through iterative denoising or unmasking rather than fixed left-to-right prediction, so local explanation may target a state of diffusion rather than a next token. We argue that this ambiguity is not merely an implementation detail, but a conceptual limitation of carrying classifier-era feature attribution directly into generative language modeling. We introduce the Attribution Contract, a specification for feature-attribution claims that names what output is being explained, which features are eligible to receive attribution, what generative process is assumed, what is held fixed, and what model score is being attributed. The contract clarifies why the same attribution method can answer different questions depending on how it is instantiated. We argue that many disagreements about feature attribution in generative language models are not disagreements about attribution algorithms, but about unstated explanatory contracts. Using autoregressive and diffusion language models as case studies, we show when attribution to earlier generated tokens, intermediate states, or denoising stages is informative, when it is misleading, and why feature-attribution methods in generative language models should be evaluated as method-contract pairs.

</details>


### [144] [ThriftAttention: Selective Mixed Precision for Long-Context FP4 Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23081)
*Joe Sharratt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThriftAttention通过两阶段策略（快速筛选重要查询-键块以FP16计算，其余块以FP4计算，并通过在线softmax合并）在长上下文任务中实现接近FP16质量但达到FP4推理效率的注意力机制。仅用5%的FP16块即可恢复平均89.1%的FP4到FP16性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Blackwell GPU的块级量化注意力（4-bit）在长上下文中导致显著的质量下降，需要一种既能保持FP4推理效率又能恢复长上下文质量的方法。

Method: 两阶段策略：1) 通过启发式方法快速选择少量重要的查询-键块对；2) 对选中的块用FP16计算，其余用FP4计算，并通过在线softmax合并两条路径的输出。

Result: 仅计算5%的查询-键块为FP16，即可恢复平均89.1%的FP4-to-FP16性能差距；优势随序列长度增加而增长，缓解了长上下文中的FP4质量退化。

Conclusion: ThriftAttention通过非均匀精度分配策略，在长上下文场景下实现了接近FP16质量与FP4效率的平衡，验证了量化误差集中在少量重要注意力块上的假设。

Abstract: Efficient attention algorithms are critical to mitigate the quadratic cost of attention in long-context workloads. Prior work utilises block-scaled quantisation techniques on Blackwell GPUs to move attention computation to 4-bit precision to accelerate inference. However, these techniques result in significant quality degradation in long-context settings. We show that the output impact of quantisation error is highly non-uniform and increases with the importance of each query-key interaction, concentrating functionally relevant error in a small number of attention blocks that contain the most important tokens. We propose ThriftAttention, a low-bit attention variant that delivers near-FP16 long-context quality at FP4 inference efficiency. This approach proceeds in two stages. First, a heuristic rapidly selects a small number of important query-key block pairs for FP16 precision. Second, the selected blocks are computed in FP16 and the remaining blocks in FP4, with both paths merged via online softmax into a single output. We demonstrate across long-context benchmarks and model families that by computing only 5% of query-key blocks in FP16, ThriftAttention recovers on average 89.1% of the FP4-to-FP16 performance gap. We show ThriftAttention's advantage grows with sequence length, mitigating the systematic FP4 quality degradation observed at longer contexts. The code is available at https://github.com/joesharratt1229/ThriftAttention.

</details>


### [145] [Dreaming Smoothly and Sample Efficiently with Gradient Penalized Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23089)
*Romil V. Sonigra,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPLD通过对DreamerV3的潜在转移动力学施加行雅可比惩罚来强制局部平滑性，从而提升基于模型的强化学习在连续控制任务中的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型（如DreamerV3）未显式强制学习到的转移动力学的局部平滑性，而这对于连续控制环境是一种有益的归纳偏置，有助于提升样本效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出GPLD正则化器，对DreamerV3的后验潜在分布施加行雅可比惩罚，并通过Hutchinson随机探针高效估计该惩罚项。该惩罚可解释为离散嵌入状态MDP中转移律有限差分平滑的连续潜在模拟。

Result: 在DeepMind Control proprioceptive任务上，GPLD提升了整体样本效率，在高复杂度运动环境中表现尤为突出。在更具挑战性的四足机器人任务上，GPLD更早达到高回报行为，并在长期训练中表现出更一致的后期学习。

Conclusion: 显式的局部平滑性正则化是一种简单有效的方法，能够改进面向平滑连续控制环境的潜在世界模型。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning improves sample efficiency by learning a world model. However, existing latent world models such as DreamerV3 do not explicitly enforce local smoothness in their learned transition dynamics, leaving a useful inductive bias for transition dynamics learning unexploited. We propose GPLD, a gradient-penalized latent dynamics regularizer for DreamerV3 that applies a row-wise Jacobian penalty to the posterior latent distribution to encourage locally smooth transition learning. We show that this penalty can be interpreted as the continuous-latent analog of finite-difference smoothing of transition laws in discrete embedded-state MDPs, and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson-style stochastic probes. Empirically, across DeepMind Control proprioceptive tasks, GPLD improves aggregate sample efficiency, with particularly strong gains on higher-complexity locomotion environments. On more challenging quadruped tasks, GPLD reaches high-return behavior earlier and exhibits more consistent late-stage learning over longer horizons. Explicit local smoothness regularization is a simple and effective way to improve latent world models for smooth continuous control environments. Code for GPLD is available at github.com/romils9/gpld-mbrl .

</details>


### [146] [Robust OT-Guided Generative Residual Domain Adaptation for Bike-Sharing Demand Prediction under Temporal Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23115)
*Yiming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随时间变化导致Citi Bike共享单车需求预测模型性能退化的问题，提出了一种基于鲁棒最优传输的残差域自适应框架Gen-ROTDA。该方法通过少量标注目标数据拟合目标域锚点、预测残差而非原始需求、生成确定性标签保持的残差特征、并修剪高成本传输匹配，在2021-2026年的3月需求预测任务中，Gen-ROTDA在2025至2026的主任务上取得最低MAE，且在跨年任务中平均表现最优。在目标域无标签异常记录下，Gen-ROTDA比非鲁棒OT变体更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于历史站点小时数据训练的共享单车模型在部署到后续年份时，由于出行模式随时间变化，模型性能会退化。现有方法未能有效处理这种时间域偏移问题。

Method: Gen-ROTDA框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 使用少量标注目标子集拟合目标域站点-时间锚点；(2) 传输残差而非原始需求；(3) 生成确定性标签保持的残差特征；(4) 修剪高成本传输匹配后训练最终残差预测器。

Result: 在2021至2026年Citi Bike 3月需求预测实验中，Gen-ROTDA在2025到2026主任务上取得最低MAE，在跨年任务中平均表现优于其他OT族方法。在目标域存在无标签异常记录时，Gen-ROTDA比非鲁棒OT变体更稳定。

Conclusion: 鲁棒最优传输对于存在噪声的时间域迁移场景下共享单车需求预测是有效的，但微调和MMD自适应仍然是具有竞争力的基线方法。

Abstract: Bike-sharing models trained on historical station-hour data may degrade when deployed in later years because travel patterns change over time. This paper studies March Citi Bike demand prediction from 2021 to 2026 as a temporal domain adaptation problem and proposes Gen-ROTDA, a robust optimal transport-guided residual domain adaptation framework. The method fits a target-domain station-time anchor with a small labeled target subset, transfers residual rather than raw demand, applies a deterministic label-preserving residual feature generator, and trims high-cost transport matches before training the final residual predictor. Experiments compare Gen-ROTDA with anchor-only, source-only, target-only, fine-tuning, MMD adaptation, Sinkhorn OTDA, ROTDA, and Gen-OTDA. Gen-ROTDA achieves the lowest MAE on the main 2025 to 2026 task and is the best OT-family method on average across multi-year tasks, although fine-tuning and MMD adaptation remain strong overall baselines. Under abnormal target-unlabeled records, Gen-ROTDA is much more stable than non-robust OT variants, suggesting that robust transport is useful for noisy temporal transfer in bike-sharing demand prediction.

</details>


### [147] [When Determinants Are Not Enough: Private Rare Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23131)
*Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了Codex如何帮助作者将稀有切换（rare switching）技术适配到差分隐私线性老虎机/强化学习设置中。标准行列式更新规则在加入高斯噪声后失去单调性，Codex提出了基于广义瑞利商的新切换规则，恢复了对数策略更新和置信度宽度比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行列式更新规则在差分隐私场景中失效——加入高斯噪声后设计矩阵不再单调增长，导致行列式增长分析无法用于遗憾界控制。

Method: 将稀有切换规则从基于行列式改为基于广义瑞利商（generalized Rayleigh quotient），从而在隐私噪声下仍保持策略更新的对数性质和置信度宽度比较。

Result: 新的切换规则恢复了对数级别的策略更新次数，并实现了与原始方法仅差常数因子的置信度宽度比较。

Conclusion: Codex辅助研究的一个成功案例，展示了AI工具在理论研究中提供创新性解决方案的潜力；同时也指出了行列式增长控制体积而遗憾分析需要控制最坏方向这一根本性差异。

Abstract: In this note, I would like to share a small research moment where Codex helped me find the right way to adapt rare switching to the private setting. The standard determinant-based update rule in linear bandits and RL works beautifully because the design matrix grows monotonically. But once Gaussian noise is added for privacy, this monotonicity can fail, and the usual analysis no longer goes through. The key reason is that determinant growth controls volume, while regret analysis needs control of the worst direction. To address this, Codex comes up with a different rare-switching rule based on the generalized Rayleigh quotient, which restores logarithmic policy updates and the desired confidence-width comparison up to a constant factor. I present my manually clean-up version of the proof here as well as some personal reflection on this example.

</details>


### [148] [CALAD: Channel-Aware contrastive Learning for multivariate time series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23139)
*Jaehyeop Hong,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALAD 提出了一种基于通道感知对比学习的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过估计通道相关性来指导对比样本构建，结合重建头和对比学习，在多个真实数据集上取得了优于现有方法的表现，尤其在分布漂移场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测方法通常对所有通道平等对待，导致异常相关信号被稀释，因为不同通道对异常检测的贡献不同。

Method: 提出 CALAD 框架：1) 基于 Transformer 自编码器的重建误差估计通道相关性；2) 利用通道相关性设计通道级数据增强策略，在正负样本构造中保留或扰动异常相关通道；3) 结合对比学习与辅助重建头，学习判别性表示同时保留正常结构。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，CALAD 持续优于现有方法，尤其在分布漂移场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通道感知的对比学习框架能有效提升多变量时间序列异常检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection has become increasingly important in real-world applications, where labeled data are often scarce. Many existing approaches rely on unsupervised learning to model normal patterns, but they often treat all channels equally. This design can dilute anomaly-relevant signals, since not all channels contribute equally to anomaly detection. In this paper, we propose CALAD, a channel-aware contrastive learning framework for multivariate time series anomaly detection. CALAD governs the construction of contrastive samples using estimated channel relevance, allowing the learning process to reflect anomaly semantics rather than generic similarity. Channel relevance is estimated from reconstruction errors of a transformer-based autoencoder and is used to distinguish channels that are more influential to anomalous behaviors. Using this information, we design a channel-wise augmentation strategy in which positive and negative samples are constructed based on whether anomaly-relevant channels are preserved or perturbed. This encourages invariance to changes in irrelevant channels while being sensitive to changes in anomaly-relevant channels. Furthermore, CALAD combines contrastive learning and an auxiliary reconstruction head, allowing the model to learn discriminative representations while retaining normal structures. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets shows that CALAD consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly under distribution shift scenarios. We provide the code for reproducibility at https://github.com/hirundo1218/CALAD

</details>


### [149] [Infra-Bayesian Reinforcement Learning Agents Outperform Classical RL For Worst-Case Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23146)
*Manish Aryal,Faiyaz Azam,Agnivo Banerjee,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Allegra Laro,Clément Legentilhomme,Andrew Lin,Florian Lorkowski,Radman Rakhshandehroo,Patric Rommel,Emanuel Ruzak,Nathan Theng,Paul Yushin Rapoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了infra-Bayesian强化学习架构在有限结果无状态决策问题中的概念验证，该架构通过维护一组不精确假设、使用infra-Bayesian条件更新、并基于最坏情况期望值选择动作，在存在Knightian不确定性和模型误设定的环境中表现出更低的最坏情况遗憾，并在Newcomb问题中优于经典决策理论智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习假设环境固定且不依赖智能体策略，但在非可实现环境中（如与预测者、人类、其他AI和机构互动），这一假设不成立，导致模型误设定下贝叶斯方法产生错误后验、不可靠决策和无界遗憾。需要一种能区分概率不确定性和Knightian不确定性并保持鲁棒性的决策框架。

Method: 实现infra-Bayesian强化学习架构：智能体维护一组不精确假设（而非单一先验），使用infra-Bayesian条件更新规则更新这些假设，通过最大化最坏情况期望值（maximin准则）选择动作。应用于有限结果无状态决策问题。

Result: 在Knightian不确定性环境中，infra-Bayesian智能体的最坏情况遗憾低于经典强化学习智能体；在Newcomb问题中，infra-Bayesian智能体选择了最优策略，优于经典决策理论智能体。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了向在模型误设定和政策依赖不确定性下保持鲁棒的强化学习智能体迈进的步骤，证明了infra-Bayesian框架在处理非可实现环境中的实际可行性。

Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning assumes the agent interacts with a fixed environment whose behavior does not depend on the agent's policy. This assumption breaks down in non-realizable settings where other actors might anticipate the agent's behavior, including environments crucial to AI safety, where the agent interacts with predictors, humans, other AI agents, and institutions. In such settings, the agent's model class fails to capture the world in which it operates. Under such misspecification, classical Bayesian methods can produce confidently wrong posteriors, unreliable decisions, and unbounded regret, as realizability fails to obtain. Infra-Bayesianism is a decision-theoretic framework that addresses these failures by distinguishing ordinary probabilistic uncertainty, where priors can be reasonably chosen, from Knightian uncertainty, where no grounds exist for the construction of such a prior. It does so by evaluating actions on their worst-case outcomes, rather than from posterior expectations or weighted averaging. We present the first proof-of-concept implementation of an infra-Bayesian reinforcement learning architecture for finite-outcome stateless decision problems. Our agent maintains a set of imprecise hypotheses, updates them using infra-Bayesian conditioning, and selects actions by maximizing worst-case expected value. We apply this implementation of the infra-Bayesian maximin decision process to an environment with Knightian uncertainty, and demonstrate a lower worst-case regret as compared to classical reinforcement learning agents. We also investigate Newcomb's problem and show that the infra-Bayesian agent picks the optimal strategy, outperforming classical decision theory agents. Our results provide a step towards reinforcement learning agents that remain robust under model misspecification and policy-dependent uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [Understanding and Improving Noisy Embedding Techniques in Instruction Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23171)
*Abhay Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymNoise方法，在嵌入层使用对称噪声微调语言模型，通过更严格约束局部曲率提升性能。在LLaMA-2-7B模型上，SymNoise在AlpacaEval上达到69.04%，相比标准方法（29.79%）和NEFTune（64.69%）均有显著提升，并在多种模型和数据集上一致优于NEFTune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEFTune在指令微调中使用均匀噪声取得了领先性能，但其优于高斯噪声的原因尚不明确。本文旨在从理论和实证两方面阐明这些噪声类型性能相当，并进一步探索更优的噪声策略。

Method: 提出SymNoise方法，在嵌入层引入对称噪声，通过更严格地约束模型的局部曲率来增强模型函数。与NEFTune使用均匀噪声不同，SymNoise采用对称噪声设计。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B模型上，标准微调得分为29.79%，NEFTune为64.69%，SymNoise达到69.04%，相对NEFTune提升6.7%。在Evol-Instruct、ShareGPT、OpenPlatypus等更强基线和多种模型上，SymNoise一致优于NEFTune。

Conclusion: SymNoise作为对噪声策略在语言模型微调中应用的进一步探索，显著超越了当前最先进的NEFTune方法，为嵌入噪声注入方向提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in instructional fine-tuning have injected noise into embeddings, with NEFTune (Jain et al., 2024) setting benchmarks using uniform noise. Despite NEFTune's empirical findings that uniform noise outperforms Gaussian noise, the reasons for this remain unclear. This paper aims to clarify this by offering a thorough analysis, both theoretical and empirical, indicating comparable performance among these noise types. Additionally, we introduce a new fine-tuning method for language models, utilizing symmetric noise in embeddings. This method aims to enhance the model's function by more stringently regulating its local curvature, demonstrating superior performance over the current method, NEFTune. When fine-tuning the LLaMA-2-7B model using Alpaca, standard techniques yield a 29.79% score on AlpacaEval. However, our approach, SymNoise, increases this score significantly to 69.04%, using symmetric noisy embeddings. This is a 6.7% improvement over the state-of-the-art method, NEFTune (64.69%). Furthermore, when tested on various models and stronger baseline instruction datasets, such as Evol-Instruct, ShareGPT, OpenPlatypus, SymNoise consistently outperforms NEFTune. The current literature, including NEFTune, has underscored the importance of more in-depth research into the application of noise-based strategies in the fine-tuning of language models. Our approach, SymNoise, is another significant step towards this direction, showing notable improvement over the existing state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [151] [Pure Exploration for a Good Policy in Reinforcement Learning with Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23182)
*Zitian Li,Wang Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了情节强化学习中的"好策略识别"（GPI）问题，该问题旨在识别一个期望奖励至少为给定阈值μ₀的策略，而非最优策略。提出了BEE-GPI算法，理论上界表明其正实例样本复杂度系数为O(H²/(V*-μ₀)²)，不依赖于状态和动作空间大小，与最优策略识别形成鲜明对比。理论下界证明了算法近乎最优性，实验验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，找到一个"足够好"的策略往往比最优策略更实用且高效。现有研究主要集中于最优策略识别（BPI），其样本复杂度通常依赖于状态和动作空间大小，而GPI问题在理论和算法上尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了BEE-GPI算法，在固定置信度设置下，以概率≥1-δ正确输出满足阈值μ₀的策略（正实例）或声明不存在（负实例），并最小化期望样本复杂度。理论分析给出了正负实例的样本复杂度上界，并建立了相匹配的下界。

Result: 正实例样本复杂度上界中log(1/δ)的系数为O(H²/(V*-μ₀)²)，该系数不依赖于状态和动作空间大小；负实例同样具有紧的样本复杂度界。下界结果证明了该系数的必要性以及BEE-GPI的近乎最优性。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GPI作为一种比BPI更宽松的识别目标，能够在样本复杂度上避免对状态和动作空间大小的依赖，在策略评估和决策中具有实际价值。BEE-GPI算法实现了几乎最优的样本效率。

Abstract: Pure exploration in episodic Reinforcement Learning has primarily focused on Best Policy Identification (BPI), which seeks to identify a (near)-optimal policy with high confidence. Motivated by practical settings where a ``good enough'' policy suffices, we study an alternate objective of Good Policy Identification (GPI). For a given reward threshold $μ_0$, GPI only requires identifying a policy with expected reward in an episode at least $μ_0$ if such a policy exists (positive instance), or declaring None if no such policy exists (negative instance). We formalize GPI under the fixed-confidence setting. We require the output to be correct with probability $\geq 1-δ$, and seek to minimize the expected sample complexity, which is the expected number of episodes explored for the output. We propose a novel algorithm BEE-GPI, and derive theoretically-grounded upper bounds on its sample complexity for positive and negative instances. Notably, for positive instances, the coefficient of $\log 1/δ$ in our upper bound is $O(H^2/(V^* - μ_0)^2)$, where $H$ is the episode length and $V^*$ is the optimal expected reward in an episode. The coefficient does not depend on the action and state space sizes otherwise, in sharp contrast to the sample complexity in BPI. We further establish lower bound results to show the near-optimality of BEE-GPI and the necessity of the $1/(V^* -μ)^2$ term. Numerical experiments further validate the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [152] [Empirical Bayes Conformal Prediction for Vision and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23189)
*Jiapeng Zeng,Yogesh Prabhu,Zhanpeng Zeng,Michael A. Newton,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于经验贝叶斯的共形预测框架，利用r值将得分变异性转化为不确定性感知的非一致性得分，在保持目标覆盖的同时减少高方差假候选的包含，并在图像分类、CLIP视觉语言模型和大型语言模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测使用单一非一致性得分，丢弃了多轮评估中的变异性信息，导致弱证据候选可能因单次偶然高分被纳入预测集。利用变异性区分稳定信号与噪声驱动的波动。

Method: 提出经验贝叶斯共形预测框架，通过r值将得分变异性转化为不确定性感知的非一致性得分。推导了闭式正态-正态经验贝叶斯估计器和非参数后验采样估计器。r值估计候选者的潜在得分在考虑均值和不确定性后属于顶级组的概率。

Result: 在图像分类、CLIP视觉语言模型基准和大型语言模型上，r值共形预测保持了目标覆盖，提高了排序稳定性，减少了集合大小。当变异性信息丰富时效果显著，变异性消失时退化为标准共形预测行为。

Conclusion: r值共形预测框架在保持分布自由覆盖保证的同时，有效利用得分变异性信息，减少了高不确定性假候选的包含，是一种更稳健的共形预测方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) gives distribution-free coverage for modern vision and language models, but it is often forced to make a ranking decision from a single unstable nonconformity score. Standard CP uses one realization, while average-then-calibrate variants smooth multiple realizations into a point estimate. Both options discard the inconsistency that can help identify whether a candidate is indeed stable. A weak answer can enter the conformal set even if the evidence is not strong, simply because one posterior sample or prompt phrasing made it look strong. But variability can help distinguish a stable signal from noise-driven fluctuations. We describe an empirical Bayes conformal prediction framework that uses $r$-values to convert score variability into an uncertainty informed nonconformity score. The resulting $r$-value estimates how likely a candidate's latent score belongs to the top-ranked group after accounting for both its mean score and its uncertainty. It admits both a closed-form Normal-Normal empirical Bayes estimator and a nonparametric posterior-sampling estimator. Using the $r$-value as the nonconformity score preserves the target conformal coverage while provably reducing the inclusion of high variance false candidates under mild regularity conditions. Across image classification, CLIP-based VLM benchmarks, and LLMs, we show that $r$-value conformal prediction preserves target coverage while improving ranking stability and reducing set size when variability is informative, and reverting to CP-like behavior when variability vanishes.

</details>


### [153] [Sample-wise Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Test-time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23411)
*Phuc Duc Nguyen,Quang Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对测试时自适应（TTA）的样本级定向后门攻击方法，通过元学习与优先级感知梯度对齐策略，在保持全局标签分布不变的情况下，仅对携带触发器的样本实施错误分类，实现高攻击成功率与低检测风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TTA定向攻击是类别级（class-wise）的，会导致目标标签频率异常升高，容易被检测。需要更现实的威胁模型——样本级定向攻击，既能成功攻击特定样本，又不破坏全局标签分布以躲避检测。

Method: 提出基于元学习的攻击框架，核心是优先级感知梯度对齐策略。将梯度更新建模为椭球置信域问题（ellipsoidal trust-region problem），在攻击成功率与分布隐蔽性之间存在梯度不对齐时，提供理论保证的优化求解。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C上跨多种TTA协议进行实验，方法实现了高定向攻击成功率，同时保持标签分布与无攻击基线一致，难以被检测，且对现有防御具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 样本级定向攻击是TTA场景下更现实且更具威胁性的攻击范式，本文提出的优先级感知梯度对齐策略有效解决了攻击成功与分布隐蔽之间的权衡问题，揭示了TTA在安全部署中的新风险。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) effectively counters distribution shifts but exposes models to adversarial manipulation via the unlabeled test stream. Existing class-wise targeted attacks remain impractical for stealthy exploitation in this setting: since TTA operates on batches, forcing a subset of samples toward a target label unintentionally pulls similar benign samples along, resulting in a conspicuously high frequency of the target label that is easy to detect. To capture a more realistic threat, we introduce a sample-wise targeted attack. Unlike prior approaches, the attacker aims to misclassify only inputs carrying an attacker-chosen trigger, while preserving the global label distribution of benign queries to evade detection. To achieve this, we propose a meta-learning-based attack with a novel priority-aware gradient alignment strategy that explicitly prioritizes attack success. The strategy formulates the gradient update as an ellipsoidal trust-region problem, mitigating the misalignment between attack success and distributional stealth, while providing theoretical guarantees for effective optimization of the attack objective in the presence of gradient misalignment. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C across TTA protocols demonstrate that our method achieves high targeted success rates while maintaining a label distribution that is consistent with the no-attack baseline, making it difficult to detect in unlabeled TTA deployment scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our attack shows strong robustness against existing defenses.

</details>


### [154] [Label-Efficient Dataset Pruning via Semi-Supervised Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23198)
*Yeseul Cho,Baekrok Shin,Changmin Kang,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemiPrune是一种标签高效的数据集剪枝框架，仅需少量随机标注子集，利用半监督学习生成伪标签，使现有监督剪枝方法能应用于未标注数据，并在目标数据集上学习以提供更可靠的难度估计和核心集选择，在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法大多需要完整标注数据，而标注成本高昂；无标签剪枝方法依赖预训练模型的特征，当目标数据集与预训练分布差异较大时不可靠。

Method: 仅使用少量随机标注子集，通过半监督学习为未标注数据生成伪标签，然后基于伪标签引发的训练动态估计样本难度，选择核心集。

Result: 在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上达到标签自由和标签高效基线中的最优性能，在标准基准上也表现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SemiPrune通过直接学习目标数据集分布，提供了更可靠的难度估计信号，能够以极低的标注成本实现有效的数据集剪枝。

Abstract: Dataset pruning reduces the storage and training costs of deep learning by selecting an informative subset from a large dataset. However, most existing pruning methods require fully labeled data, which limits their applicability in realistic settings where unlabeled data are abundant and annotation is costly. Recent label-free pruning methods address this issue, but they rely on features from pretrained models to estimate example difficulty. This dependence can be unreliable when the target dataset differs substantially from the pretraining distribution. We propose SemiPrune, a label-efficient dataset pruning framework, using only a small randomly labeled subset, that uses semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled data, allowing existing supervised pruning methods that require label information to be seamlessly applied to the resulting pseudo-labeled training pool. We then estimate example difficulty from pseudo-label-induced training dynamics and select a coreset. By learning directly from the target dataset, our method better captures the target distribution and provides more reliable signals for difficulty estimation and coreset selection. We validate our approach on domain-specific, image-corrupted, and long-tailed datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance among label-free and label-efficient baselines, while also demonstrating competitive performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Instance-Optimal Estimation with Multiple LLM Judges on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23362)
*Junghyun Lee,Sanghwa Kim,Yassir Jedra,Alexandre Proutière,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在预算约束下，通过自适应分配评估查询给不同成本与可靠性的LLM裁判，以最小化评估误差的最优方法（EST-IVWE），并证明了其达到局部极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估范式虽广泛使用但成本高昂：不同裁判定价与可靠性各异，且不同提示-响应对的难度差异大。如何在固定预算下最优分配查询以最准确地估计分数，是核心动机。

Method: 形式上化为预算异方差多裁判估计问题。提出逆方差加权估计量（IVWE）及其基于乐观偏差方差估计的自适应算法EST-IVWE。使用Assouad型期望论证（而非Fano型高概率论证）推导局部极小极大下界。

Result: EST-IVWE的误差率在预算上达到oracle IVWE率（仅低阶项差异）；推导了匹配的局部极小极大下界，证明算法实例最优性；数值实验在合成数据和HelpSteer2上优于朴素均匀分配。

Conclusion: 所提方法在预算约束下实现了评估查询的最优分配，理论完备且实践有效。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models increasingly relies on LLM-as-a-judge protocols, but such evaluations remain costly: different judges have different prices and reliabilities, and the difficulty of each prompt-response pair can vary substantially. This raises a basic allocation question: under a fixed budget, how should one distribute evaluation queries across heterogeneous judges and instances to obtain the most accurate score estimates? We formalize this question as *budgeted heteroskedastic multi-judge estimation*. Given $K$ prompt-response pairs, $J$ judges with known costs, and unknown query-judge variances, the goal is to estimate a bounded score vector while minimizing an $\ell_p$-error. Our first contribution is to analyze the inverse-variance weighted estimator (IVWE) and to derive the oracle allocation that minimizes its error rate. Since this allocation depends on the unknown variances, we then address the practical unknown-variance setting by proposing EST-IVWE, an adaptive algorithm that constructs and leverages *optimistically biased* variance estimates to stabilize the empirical allocation. We prove that EST-IVWE matches the oracle IVWE rate up to lower-order terms in the budget. Our second and central theoretical contribution is a matching *local* minimax lower bound, which establishes the instance-optimality of the proposed algorithms. A key technical insight is that Fano-type high-probability arguments are too coarse for this problem: their packing construction loses the local variance structure that governs the optimal allocation. We instead use an Assouad-type in-expectation argument, based on local perturbations, which preserves this structure and yields the sharp allocation-dependent lower bound. Finally, we numerically validate the superiority of our approach over naïve uniform allocation on synthetic and HelpSteer2 datasets.

</details>


### [156] [FastKernels: Benchmarking GPU Kernel Generation in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23215)
*Gabriele Oliaro,Yichao Fu,May Jiang,Owen Lu,Junli Wang,Zhihao Jia,Hao Zhang,Samyam Rajbhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有GPU kernel基准测试与生产推理框架严重脱节，导致基于LLM的kernel agent产生误导性优化信号。本文提出FastKernels，一个包含46个代表性架构、覆盖96.2% HuggingFace Transformer架构的基准测试，同时兼具生产级推理框架能力。评估表明，最强kernel agent仅实现0.94倍加速，证实基准-生产错位是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU kernel基准测试存在三大问题：1) 在单GPU上用合成输入评估，忽略实际编译栈；2) 奖励复制已知优化而非发现新优化；3) 生成的kernel在沙盒中表现良好，但集成到真实系统时会出现接口不兼容、编译栈冲突和正确性退化。这些问题导致agent学习到误导性的reward信号，严重制约了LLM-based kernel agent的进步。

Method: FastKernels构建于最小化但代表性的46个架构（覆盖8个类别），其kernel子sums了96.2%的HuggingFace Transformer架构。同时作为生产级推理框架，与vLLM、SGLang等系统性能持平，在未被充分服务的架构上显著超越上游参考。每个任务的接口与其架构族最先进库的对应模块镜像，可直接部署优化后的kernel到生产代码库。

Result: 评估最强kernel agent仅达到0.94倍聚合加速比（相对于生产基线），较弱的agent分别为0.78倍和0.53倍，证实了基准-生产错位是当前领域的核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: FastKernels作为基准测试与生产级框架的统一体，旨在消除benchmark增益与生产吞吐提升之间的鸿沟，推动kernel agent向可直接转化为实际收益的方向发展。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for GPU kernel generation are advancing rapidly, yet their progress is fundamentally constrained by the benchmarks they optimize against. Existing benchmarks are poorly aligned with production inference frameworks: they evaluate kernels on a single GPU with synthetic inputs, ignore the surrounding compilation stack, and reward replicating known optimizations rather than discovering new ones. The resulting reward signals are misleading: agents learn to generate kernels that score well in sandboxes but introduce interface incompatibilities, compilation-stack conflicts, and silent correctness degradation when integrated into real systems. We introduce FastKernels, a kernel benchmark built around a minimal set of 46 representative architectures spanning 8 categories, whose kernels collectively subsume those of 96.2% (409/425) of HuggingFace Transformers architectures. FastKernels doubles as a minimalistic, production-grade inference framework that runs at parity with hardened systems such as vLLM and SGLang on mainstream LLM serving and substantially exceeds upstream references on under-served architectures; each task's interface mirrors the corresponding module in the state-of-the-art library for its architecture family, enabling direct deployment of optimized kernels into production codebases. Evaluating state-of-the-art kernel agents on FastKernels, we find that even the strongest agent achieves only 0.94$\times$ aggregate speedup over production baselines, with weaker agents at $0.78\times$ and $0.53\times$ -- confirming that benchmark-production misalignment is a critical bottleneck for the field. We release FastKernels as a stepping stone toward kernel agents whose benchmark gains translate directly into production throughput improvements. Code is available at https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/fastkernels

</details>


### [157] [PaP-NF: Probabilistic Long-Term Time Series Forecasting via Prefix-as-Prompt Reprogramming and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23219)
*Minju Kim,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PaP-NF概率预测框架，通过Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与大语言模型对齐，并利用归一化流解码器生成概率分布，在长期预测基准上有效捕捉多模态不确定性同时保持点预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法多依赖确定性模型，无法捕捉现实环境中固有的不确定性和复杂未来行为，单点预测不足以满足需求，因此需要概率预测方法来量化和表示不确定性。

Method: 采用Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型对齐，并利用归一化流解码器以LLM提取的全局上下文为条件生成预测分布。

Result: 在多种长期预测基准上，PaP-NF能够稳健地捕捉多模态不确定性，同时保持具有竞争力的点预测精度。

Conclusion: PaP-NF作为一种概率预测框架，成功结合了大语言模型的表征能力和归一化流的灵活性，有效提升了时间序列预测中的不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a central role in many real-world applications and has been extensively studied. Most existing approaches rely on deterministic models. However, real-world environments exhibit inherently uncertain and complex future behaviors, making single-point predictions insufficient. This highlights the need for probabilistic forecasting methods that can quantify and represent uncertainty. In this work, we propose PaP-NF, a probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns continuous time series representations with a frozen large language model (LLM) using a Prefix-as-Prompt mechanism, and conditions a normalizing flow decoder on the global context extracted by the LLM. The quality of the resulting predictive distributions is evaluated using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS), a standard metric in probabilistic forecasting. Across a variety of long-term forecasting benchmarks, PaP-NF robustly captures multi-modal uncertainty while maintaining competitive point forecasting accuracy. The official implementation is available at: https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF

</details>


### [158] [WMAttack: Automated Attack Search for Adversarial Evaluation of World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23220)
*Zhixiang Guo,Siyuan Liang,Shi Fu,Cheng Guo,Andras Balogh,Mark Jelasity,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMAttack，一个面向世界模型智能体鲁棒性评估的自动化对抗攻击搜索框架。通过自修正攻击搜索(SCAS)和表征引导攻击检索(RGAR)两大模块，在有限预算下高效发现更强的攻击策略。在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack显著优于现有基线方法，将DreamerV3 Atari上的归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，DMC上从0.319提升至0.682。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为决策智能体的应用日益广泛，但其对抗鲁棒性因缺乏自动化评估方法而未被充分探索。现有评估面临两难：手工调参的攻击可能低估鲁棒性，而穷举搜索因需要在学习到的潜动态上进行闭环 rollout 而代价过高。

Method: WMAttack将鲁棒性评估建模为有限预算下对攻击配置（攻击家族、扰动预算、优化步数、重启次数、分配规则）的搜索问题。核心包含两个模块：(1) 自修正攻击搜索(SCAS)，利用奖励下降、动作不稳定、运行时开销和 rollout 变异性等反馈信号迭代优化攻击提议分布；(2) 表征引导攻击检索(RGAR)，从表征相似任务中检索历史有效配置，为未见环境提供热启动。同时提供理论分析说明提议分布修正如何提升有限预算搜索效率。

Result: 在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack一致发现比基线更强的攻击：DreamerV3 Atari上归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，DMC上从0.319提升至0.682。消融实验表明RGAR提升初始候选质量，SCAS在固定评估预算下提升最终攻击效用。

Conclusion: WMAttack作为首个面向世界模型智能体的自动化对抗评估框架，有效解决了准确性与效率之间的权衡问题。理论分析和实验验证均表明其有效性，为世界模型的鲁棒性评估提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of world models as decision-making agents, their adversarial robustness remains underexplored due to the lack of dedicated automated evaluation methods. A key obstacle is that attack evaluation must be both accurate and efficient: weak manually tuned attacks can overestimate robustness, while exhaustive hyperparameter search is prohibitively expensive because each candidate requires closed-loop rollouts through learned latent dynamics. We introduce WMAttack, an automated attack-search framework for adversarial evaluation of world-model agents. WMAttack formulates robustness evaluation as a finite-budget search over attack configurations, including attack families, perturbation budgets, optimization steps, restarts, and allocation rules. To improve search accuracy, Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) refines the attack proposal distribution using feedback from reward degradation, action instability, runtime cost, and rollout variability. To improve search efficiency, Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) retrieves effective historical configurations from representation-similar tasks, providing a warm start for unseen environments. We provide a theoretical explanation showing that proposal refinement improves finite-budget search when it shifts probability mass toward high-utility attacks. Across Atari and DeepMind Control tasks, WMAttack consistently discovers stronger attacks than the evaluated baselines, improving normalized reward drop from 0.497 to 1.034 on DreamerV3 Atari and from 0.319 to 0.682 on DMC. Ablations further show that RGAR improves initial candidate quality and SCAS improves final attack utility under fixed evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [159] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将空间公平性从单一居住地扩展到个体移动模式，并设计了一种基于空间扫描统计的方法来检测预测模型在移动模式上的不公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间公平性研究仅基于个体单一居住地评估模型公平性，忽略了个体在多区域间移动模式也可能受到不公平对待的问题。

Method: 首先将个体移动轨迹关联到不同地理区域，考虑多种空间分区（不同分辨率和对齐方式），然后使用合适的空间扫描统计量来评估预测模型是否在移动模式上表现出不公平性。

Result: 在数千个合成不公平数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效检测这种新型不公平性，并能检索出受到不公平对待的对象集合；定位性能呈现一致的多分辨率权衡。

Conclusion: 需要将空间公平性的概念从单一居住地扩展到移动模式；提出的方法能有效检测移动模式层面的不公平性，但存在多分辨率权衡问题。

Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

</details>


### [160] [Convex Low-resource Accent-Robust Language Detection in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23235)
*Miria Feng,William Tan,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，将凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统中，通过多GPU ADMM算法在JAX中高效实现，在低资源方言变体检测任务上达到97-98%准确率，并提供了全局最优性保证和理论稳定性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前口语对话系统在处理低资源方言和口音时频繁失败，常误识别输入语言，导致下游对话任务级联失效。标准微调方法计算开销大且易在高维语音数据上过拟合，亟需一种高效、鲁棒的方言检测方法。

Method: 提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，将凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统流程中；通过多GPU交替方向乘子法（ADMM）在JAX中高效实现，实现多项式时间内的快速训练并保证全局最优性。

Result: 在低资源场景下达到97-98%的方言检测准确率，展现出样本效率和对抗输入方言变体的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 凸优化方法能有效解决低资源方言检测问题，CLD框架兼具理论保证（全局最优性、边际稳定性、特征扰动鲁棒性）与实证优势，为口语对话系统的语言识别提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Globalization and multiculturalism continue to produce increasingly diverse speech varieties. Yet current spoken dialogue systems frequently fail on under-represented dialects and accents, often misidentifying the input language and causing cascading failures in downstream dialogue tasks. Addressing this dialectal variance under low-resource constraints remains an open challenge, as standard fine-tuning is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting on high-dimensional speech data. We propose Convex Language Detection (CLD), a novel framework that integrates theoretically grounded convex optimization techniques into the spoken dialogue systems pipeline. Our method is efficiently implemented via multi-GPU Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) in JAX, thus providing global optimality guarantees and fast training in polynomial time. Theoretically, we prove that our convex objective induces certified margin stability and provide guarantees against feature perturbations. Empirically, we demonstrate sample efficiency and robustness to input dialectical variation, achieving 97-98% accuracy in challenging low-resource regimes. Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

</details>


### [161] [Self-supervised Adversarial Purification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23239)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GPR-GAE，一种基于自监督图自编码器的对抗净化框架，通过广义PageRank滤波器实现结构感知的图数据净化，在分类前独立去除对抗扰动，达到鲁棒性与准确性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗训练方法将鲁棒性和准确性纠缠在同一分类器中，难以平衡二者；现有对抗净化方法缺乏对多样图结构的自适应能力。

Method: 提出GPR-GAE作为专用净化器，采用自监督策略训练；利用多个广义PageRank（GPR）滤波器捕获多样化的结构表示；设计多步净化过程实现精确的图恢复。

Result: 在多个数据集和攻击场景下达到最先进的鲁棒性，可作为即插即用的独立净化器。

Conclusion: GPR-GAE有效分离鲁棒性与分类任务，通过自监督图净化实现对抗防御，验证了独立净化器范式的有效性。

Abstract: Defending Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) against adversarial attacks requires balancing accuracy and robustness, a trade-off often mishandled by traditional methods like adversarial training that intertwine these conflicting objectives within a single classifier. To overcome this limitation, we propose a self-supervised adversarial purification framework. We separate robustness from the classifier by introducing a dedicated purifier, which cleanses the input data before classification. In contrast to prior adversarial purification methods, we propose GPR-GAE, a novel graph auto-encoder (GAE), as a specialized purifier trained with a self-supervised strategy, adapting to diverse graph structures in a data-driven manner. Utilizing multiple Generalized PageRank (GPR) filters, GPR-GAE captures diverse structural representations for robust and effective purification. Our multi-step purification process further facilitates GPR-GAE to achieve precise graph recovery and robust defense against structural perturbations. Experiments across diverse datasets and attack scenarios demonstrate the state-of-the-art robustness of GPR-GAE, showcasing it as an independent plug-and-play purifier for GNN classifiers. Our code can be found at https://github.com/woodavid31/GPR-GAE.

</details>


### [162] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RelPrism提出了一种多面自监督学习框架，通过构造内在、关系和混合三种属性视角，并进行多粒度聚类形成伪任务池进行预训练，从而提升关系数据库下游任务的表示学习效果。在5个真实数据集的14个任务上，分类任务ROC-AUC提升4.15%，回归任务MAE降低10.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系数据库的自监督学习方法通常只从单一视角（如节点级内在属性或子图级关系结构）构造预训练信号，无法覆盖下游任务所需的多维度、多粒度信息，限制了表示学习的适应性和泛化能力。

Method: RelPrism首先从关系数据库中提取内在属性、关系属性和混合属性三种视角；然后对每种视角应用多粒度聚类，构建相应粒度的伪任务；最后在这些伪任务池上进行预训练，使模型暴露于更广泛的视角和粒度级别，从而获得更强的下游适应能力。

Result: 在5个真实数据集的14个任务上，RelPrism在分类任务中ROC-AUC提升4.15%，在回归任务中MAE降低10.75%，超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过多面多粒度的自监督预训练，RelPrism有效解决了关系数据库表示学习中单一视角监督信号不足的问题，显著提升了各类下游任务的性能。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

</details>


### [163] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究学习增强调度中限制抢占次数与优化延迟之间的权衡，提出在单机和无关并行机上仅需O(1)次抢占/任务即可实现O(1)竞争比的算法，且开销随预测误差对数增长，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习增强调度算法主要通过频繁的"盲目"抢占来优化任务延迟，忽略了算法性能与抢占复杂度之间的根本性权衡。本文旨在弥补这一空白，建立限制抢占次数同时优化延迟的理论基础。

Method: 提出O(1)竞争比算法，在单机和无关并行机上仅需O(1)次抢占/任务；抢占开销随预测误差对数增长；首次将有限抢占保证拓展到无关机器和可延展机器场景。

Result: 在准确预测下实现O(1)竞争比且仅需O(1)次抢占/任务；抢占开销随预测误差对数增长；为无关机器和可延展机器提供了首个有限抢占保证。

Conclusion: 学习增强框架能够有效弥合理论延迟界与抢占开销之间的差距，并可将有限抢占保证拓展到更受限且更现实的设置中。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations. Our results include $O(1)$-competitive algorithms for single and unrelated parallel machines with only $O(1)$ preemptions per job under accurate predictions, with overhead scaling logarithmically with the prediction error. By providing the first bounded-preemption guarantees for unrelated and malleable machines, we extend the theoretical reach of the learning-augmented framework to more constrained and realistic settings. Finally, our algorithms are validated through experiments.

</details>


### [164] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR提出了一种三路令牌路由机制（保留、近似、驱逐），通过结合重要性信号和可重构性信号，在基于驱逐的KV缓存压缩管线中恢复有价值的信息，改善了中高压缩率下的质量-内存权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于二值驱逐或表示近似的KV缓存压缩方法未能充分利用那些虽非关键但可重构的令牌，导致信息不可逆丢失。

Method: VECTOR在驱逐管线中引入三路令牌路由：保留、近似、驱逐。结合基础评分器的重要性信号和离线校准的回归值估计的可重构性信号，对令牌进行三类路由决策。

Result: 在中高压缩率下质量-内存权衡得到改善，在更严格的预算条件下增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 通过可重构性信号恢复有用值信息，同时保留关键向量以保证注意力路由稳定性，VECTOR作为即插即用增强方案有效提升了驱逐型管线的性能。

Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

</details>


### [165] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对符号回归中"好结构、坏评分"这一关键瓶颈，提出了一种利用符号表达式结构和语义先验的SR原生拟合框架SAGE-Fit，作为即插即用模块可显著提升评估保真度并普遍改善多种SR系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法采用双层优化框架，但非线性算子导致内层参数优化高度非凸，依赖快速局部求解器（如BFGS）常陷入不良局部最小值，使得正确结构被低估评分（"Good Structure, Bad Score"现象），严重误导外层搜索并降低效率。解决这一优化瓶颈是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出SAGE-Fit框架，利用符号表达式的双重原生先验——结构先验和语义先验，分别设计定制化模块来处理每种属性，从而缓解参数拟合中的非凸优化瓶颈。

Result: 大量实验表明，SAGE-Fit作为即插即用模块，显著提升了评估保真度，并普遍改善了多种符号回归系统的性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用符号表达式特有的结构和语义先验，所提方法有效解决了符号回归中的关键优化瓶颈，为提升SR系统的评估准确性和搜索效率提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

</details>


### [166] [Symbolic Density Estimation for Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21813)
*Ziwen Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出符号密度估计（SDE）框架，能够自动从数据中恢复封闭形式的离散概率质量函数，结合结构化先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推理，在基准数据集和实际应用中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散概率定律是统计建模的基础，但可解释分布目录的增长缓慢且依赖逐案例的数学推导，缺乏自动发现新分布的方法。

Method: 提出符号密度估计（SDE）框架，在结构化搜索空间中通过组合基本解析操作自动恢复封闭形式的概率质量函数，集成领域特定的结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推理阶段，并扩展到零膨胀和有限混合等更丰富的分布族。

Result: 在涵盖广泛常用离散分布的基准数据集上，SDE恢复了所有基准分布族并给出了准确的参数估计；在真实数据应用中，识别出简洁且可解释的混合模型，改进了拟合优度。

Conclusion: SDE能自动发现可解释的离散概率分布，扩展了分布族，并在真实数据中优于标准模型，为自动统计建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Discrete probability laws underpin statistical modeling, yet the catalog of interpretable distributions has expanded only gradually through centuries of case-by-case mathematical derivations. We introduce symbolic density estimation (SDE), an unsupervised framework that automatically recovers closed-form probability mass functions by composing elementary analytic operations within a structured search space. Our method integrates domain-specific structural priors with evolutionary search and a validity-aware inference stage, and it extends to richer distribution families such as zero inflation and finite mixtures. To support systematic evaluation and future research, we contribute a benchmark dataset spanning a broad collection of commonly used discrete distributions. The proposed algorithm recovers all benchmark families with accurate parameter estimates. A real data application shows that it identifies concise and interpretable mixture models that improve goodness-of-fit over standard models.

</details>


### [167] [Optimal Dimension-Free Sampling for Regularized Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23726)
*Meysam Alishahi,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一类Lipschitz连续分类损失函数（包括逻辑损失、Sigmoid损失、Hinge损失和ReLU损失等）在多种正则化项下证明了达到(1±ε)相对误差的最优采样界。对于ℓ₂/k正则化得到k²/ε²的上下界，对于ℓ₁/k正则化得到k/ε²的上下界，并且通过更精细的矩界和经验过程分析，改进了近期基于敏感性采样的k³/ε²界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Lipschitz连续分类损失函数在多种正则化项下建立严格且最优的采样复杂度界，填补现有理论空白，并改进近期敏感性采样方法中次优的立方复杂度界限。

Method: 采用简单的均匀采样或（平方）范数采样，结合高阶矩界和经验过程分析来避免VC维和敏感性框架中常见的重复计数问题，从而得到更紧的上下界。

Result: ℓ₂/k正则化下得到k²/ε²的上下界；ℓ₁/k正则化下得到k/ε²的上下界；ℓ₂²/k正则化下若满足有界导数性质|g'(x)|≤g(x)且g(0)>0且g单调或凸，则具有线性k的采样复杂度，否则为k²/ε²；若g(0)=0则不可能有维度无关界。

Conclusion: 本文为Lipschitz连续分类损失函数建立了最优的采样复杂度理论，所提出的方法在理论上优于近期敏感性采样方法，且所有上界均由匹配的下界（至多相差多项式对数项）所补充。

Abstract: We prove optimal sampling bounds achieving $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-relative error for a broad class of Lipschitz continuous classification loss functions under various regularization terms. This includes important functions such as logistic and sigmoid loss, hinge loss, and ReLU loss, as prominent and popular representative examples. In particular, we prove $k^2/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_2/k$ regularization, and $k/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_1/k$ regularization. For $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$ regularization, the sampling complexity depends mainly on a bounded derivative property: if $|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$, and $g(0)>0$, and $g$ is monotonic or convex, then it admits linear in $k$ sampling complexity; otherwise the general bound is $k^2/\varepsilon^2$. However, if $g(0)=0$, our results indicate that no dimension-free bounds are possible, and even sublinear bounds are ruled out. All upper bounds are complemented by matching lower bounds up to polylogarithmic terms. Moreover, our work relies conceptually and algorithmically on simple uniform or (squared) norm sampling and hereby improves over recent cubic $k^3/\varepsilon^2$ sensitivity sampling bounds of (Alishahi and Phillips, ICML'24). This is achieved by refined arguments involving higher moment bounds and empirical process analyses to avoid overcounting that appears in the de-facto standard VC-dimension and sensitivity framework.

</details>


### [168] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数先验映射（PPM）框架，通过将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程，在概率多变量时间序列预测中兼顾参数化方法的效率与生成模型的表达能力，实现对非平稳动态的高效建模，在准确性和计算效率上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，参数化方法具备强归纳偏置但灵活性不足，而深度生成模型虽表达能力强却需要大量数据和计算资源才能捕捉复杂时间依赖关系。为平衡表达力与鲁棒性，需要一种新框架来融合两者的优势。

Method: PPM框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 参数化估计器，用于推导动态自适应的先验分布；(2) 可学习映射，将该参数化先验引导至复杂预测分布的学习过程。模型通过混合目标函数进行训练，同时优化预测精度和不确定性校准。

Result: 在非平稳数据上，PPM在预测精度和计算效率之间取得了更优的权衡，不确定性校准良好，整体性能超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 将参数化先验映射到生成模型是一种有效的策略，既能保留参数方法的计算效率，又能发挥生成模型的表达优势，特别适用于非平稳多变量时间序列预测任务。

Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping. This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

</details>


### [169] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的循环Transformer方法，通过在推理时对冻结的预训练模型中间层块进行循环包装，使用阻尼子步替代大更新，在多个模型上取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环Transformer方法需要在循环结构下进行端到端训练或微调，计算成本高。本文希望在免训练的情况下将循环机制引入预训练模型，以提升推理性能。

Method: 在推理时，将预训练Transformer的连续中间层块作为循环体进行重复应用；为防止性能退化，将单次大更新替换为多个阻尼子步（damped sub-steps），灵感来源于将预归一化Transformer块视为ODE的前向欧拉步。

Result: 在7个模型家族（包括密集、稀疏MoE、MLA+MoE）上均有提升：Qwen3-4B-Instruct在MMLU-Pro上+2.64pp，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct在CommonsenseQA上+1.14pp，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct在OpenBookQA上+1.20pp。

Conclusion: 无需额外训练的循环策略可以有效提升冻结预训练模型的推理性能，循环的应用策略（阻尼子步）至关重要，简单重复块会导致性能下降。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [170] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典修正扩散模型，用于气象统计降尺度。该模型在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈层插入变分量子电路层，保持回归分支完全经典。实验表明，混合模型在2020验证集上保持稳定，保留大尺度空间组织，并在多个配置下改进了MAE和CRPS指标。结构诊断显示混合变体能保持动能谱和风速分布，同时控制尾部行为、极端风速定位和联合风场分量结构的变化。后端研究显示模拟设备噪声影响可忽略，但真实硬件部署受限于量子比特可用性和执行保真度。2021年分布外测试表明这些增益不能均匀迁移，揭示了泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子电路作为紧凑非线性特征映射在潜通道混合中的可行性，以提升统计降尺度性能，同时评估其在实际硬件部署中的局限性。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典修正扩散模型，在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈层插入变分量子电路层，包括通道内和跨通道两种量子电路架构，用于10米风场数据的统计降尺度。

Result: 混合模型在2020验证集上改进了MAE和CRPS指标，保持动能谱和风速分布，控制尾部行为和极端风定位。模拟噪声影响可忽略，但真实硬件受限。2021年分布外测试显示泛化差距。

Conclusion: 瓶颈级量子混合可以为气象统计降尺度做出有意义的贡献，但电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素；需要通过稳定化和正则化来弥合泛化差距。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure. Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

</details>


### [171] [Reflex: Reinforcement Learning with Reflection Symmetry Exploitation in State-Based Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23415)
*Shuai Zhen,Yifan Zhang,Yuling Wang,Yanhua Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex框架，通过将反射对称性（轴向反射和双边反射）融入强化学习策略学习，显著提升状态连续控制任务的样本效率，在PPO和SAC算法上验证了优于基线方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习长期面临样本效率低的问题，现有利用群不变马尔可夫决策过程的工作主要集中于图像RL和旋转对称性（如SO(2)），而基于状态的RL和反射对称性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 形式化定义了两种反射对称类型（轴向反射和双边反射）及其变换；基于对称性保持的最优值函数和策略的理论分析，通过原则性的对称性正则化机制将反射对称性集成到策略学习中；与PPO和SAC算法无缝集成。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准测试套件上，Reflex在PPO和SAC的集成版本均优于标准基线，同时提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 反射对称性可以有效提升状态连续控制任务的样本效率，所提出的Reflex框架能够通用地兼容同策略和异策略RL算法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has long struggled with poor sample efficiency. One promising approach to mitigate this problem is leveraging group-invariant Markov Decision Processes ($G$-invariant MDPs). Existing works in this direction have primarily focused on image-based RL and rotational symmetry such as $\mathrm{SO(2)}$, leaving state-based RL and reflection symmetry largely underexplored. In this work, we focus on state-based continuous control tasks and exploit reflection symmetry by introducing Reflex, a paradigm that seamlessly integrates with both on-policy and off-policy RL algorithms. We formalize two types of reflection-axial reflection and bilateral reflection, and characterize their corresponding transformations. Building on a theoretical analysis of symmetry-preserving optimal value functions and policies, Reflex integrates reflection symmetry into policy learning through principled symmetry regularization mechanisms. We integrate Reflex with PPO and SAC, and evaluate it on a suite of OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance over standard baselines while improving sample efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex.

</details>


### [172] [An Open-Source Training Dataset for Foundation Models for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23417)
*Aaron Klein,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Luca Thale-Bombien,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BBO-Pile，首个大规模公开黑箱优化轨迹数据集（含500K+轨迹、3095个黑箱问题），并基于该数据集训练了从2M到80M参数的系列基础模型，验证了大规模预训练在黑箱优化领域的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法依赖大量超参数调优，泛化能力有限；基础模型方法虽有潜力但此前缺乏大规模、真实世界、公开可用的预训练数据集，限制了该领域的可复现性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建BBO-Pile数据集（500K+优化轨迹、3095个黑箱问题），训练多尺度基础模型（2M-80M参数，200M-2B tokens），研究模型规模与计算量的缩放行为。

Result: 大规模预训练能够有效模仿黑箱优化方法，模型性能随计算量增长呈现可预测的缩放规律。

Conclusion: BBO-Pile数据集和大规模预训练为黑箱优化基础模型研究提供了可行路径，推动了该方向的进一步发展。

Abstract: Most black-box optimization methods require extensive hyperparameter tuning, often limiting their ability to generalize across different optimization domains. Foundation models for black-box optimization that learn optimization principles from a large collection of optimization trajectories offer a promising alternative, with the potential to outperform manually designed methods across diverse problem classes. However, prior work has either relied on non-public datasets or on purely synthetic data, limiting reproducibility and generalization to real-world problems.
